iccoṟkaḷ ellām āṅkilattiṉiṉṟum tamiḻukku vantavai yalla. āṅkilam varumuṉṉarē ivai tamiḻil uḷḷaṉa. āṟā yiram kalliṭaiyiṭṭa āṅkilattiṟkum tamiḻukkum ittuṇaip potuc coṟkaḷirukkumāyiṉ. ēṟattāḻa 5,000 āṇṭukaḷāka aṭuttaṭuttu vaḻaṅkum vaṭamoḻikkum teṉmoḻikkum ettuṇaip potuc coṟkaḷiruttal vēṇṭum? ippotuc coṟkaḷellām moḻi nūṉ muṟaiyil kūrntu nōkku vārkkut tūya teṉcoṟkaḷeṉṟē tōṉṟā niṟkum, vaṭa moḻikkum teṉmoḻikkum potuvāyuḷḷa coṟkaḷai, vaṭacolleṉpārum teṉ colleṉpārumām irucārār moḻi nūlaṟiñar. vaṭamoḻiyaip pōṉṟē teṉmoḻiyum ciṟantatoru tāymoḻiyātaliṉ, vaṭamoḻiyiṉiṉṟum teṉmoḻikku vantuḷḷa coṟkaḷaip pōlavē teṉmoḻi yiṉiṉṟum vaṭamoḻikkuc ceṉṟuḷḷa coṟkaḷum paṉṉūṟ ṟukkaṇakkiṉa. avaṟṟuḷ iraṇṭu ilakkiyam, ilakkaṇam eṉpaṉa.  (tamiḻppoḻil viṭai 1934)
ilakkaṇavurai vaḻukkaḷ
1.	oṟṟaḷapeṭai
ṅañaṇa namaṉa  eṉṉum naṉṉūṟ cūttirattiṟku.
cūttirattuk kūṟappaṭṭa pattoṟṟum āytam oṉṟumākap patiṉō reḻuttum, kuṟiṟkīḻiṭai, kuṟiliṇaik kīḻiṭai, kuṟiṟ kīḻikkaṭai, kuṟiliṇaik kīḻkkaṭai eṉṉum nāṉkiṭattum aḷapeṭukka oṟṟaḷa peṭai mottam nāṟpattu nāṉkām. avaṟṟuḷ āyta māṉatu iyalpīṟāka vēṉum vitiyīṟāka vēṉum colluk kīṟṟil vārāmaiyāl kuṟiṟ kīḻkkaṭai kuṟilik kīḻkkaṭai eṉṉum iraṇṭiṭattaiyuṅ kaḻikka oṟṟaḷapeṭai nāṟpat tiraṇṭātal kāṇka eṉṟē muṉṉaip piṉṉai uraiyāciriya rellām uraittār.
kuṟṟiyalukaram kuṟiṟkīḻaṉṟik kuṟiliṇaikkīḻ vārāteṉpatu.
kuṟiyataṉ muṉṉa rāytap puḷḷi
uyiroṭu puṇarnta vallāṟaṉ micaittē
eṉṉum cūttirattāṟ ṟeḷivām. ataṉ uraiyilum.
vilaḵḵki vīṅkiru ḷōṭṭumē māta
rilaḵḵku mutti ṉiṉam
eṉa ivvāṟu kuṟiliṇaik kīḻum āytam varumālōveṉiṉ, aṉṉa iyalpākavē āytamāy niṟpaṉavalla, ōcai niṟaittaṟ poruṭṭu oṟṟilvaḻi oṟṟākkutaliṉ, īṇṭu āytamum oṟṟāy varumeṉak koḷka eṉṟār mayilainātar. ākavē kuṟiliṇaikkīḻ āytam vārāteṉpatu peṟappaṭṭatu.
iṉi,  al + tiṇai = aḵṟiṇai, muḷ + tītu = muḵṭītu. āv + kaṭiya = aḵkaṭiya eṉa vitiyīṟāy āytam varutaliṉ īṟṟil vārāteṉpatum pōliyuraiyeṉa maṟukkappaṭum. ākavē, āytam kuṟiṟkīḻiṭai kuṟiṟkīḻkkaṭai eṉṉum īriṭattum vantu aḷapeḻum eṉṟum, kuṟiliṇaik kīḻiṭai kuṟiliṇaik kīḻkkaṭai eṉṉumīriṭattum vārāteṉṟum, av viraṇṭiṭat taiyumē kaḻikka oṟṟaḷa peṭai mottam nāṟpattiraṇṭām eṉṟum kaṇṭukoḷka.
2.	ēvalorumai
naṭavā maṭicī eṉṉum ēvalviṉaic cūttira uraiyil cey eṉṉum pakutiyoṭu āy vikuti puṇarntu ceyyāy eṉa niṉṟu. piṉpu avvikuti keṭṭu mīṇṭum cey eṉa niṟpatē ēvalorumai yeṉac cilar uraittār.
vikuti puṇarntu keṭuvatu iṅkilīcu camaṟkirutam pōṉṟa āriyamoḻikaṭ kiyalpēyaṉṟit tamiḻukkiyalpākātu. Cut, set  eṉṉum viṉaikaḷ iṟanta kālattil vikuti peṟṟiḻantu mīṇṭum pakutiyaḷavākavēyirukkum.
vikuti puṇarntu keṭuvatāyiṉ, aṅṅaṉam keṭāta cila viṉaikaḷumiruttal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉa miṉmaiyiṉ cey eṉṉum vāypāṭē ēvalorumai yeṉṟu tuṇiyappaṭum.
mēlum, ceyyāy eṉpatu muṉṉilai etirmaṟai orumai etirkāla viṉaimuṟṟuc col. atu ōcai vēṟupāṭṭāṉ vēṇṭu kōḷāṅkāl ceyyāyā eṉa viṉāpporuḷ paṭṭup piṉpu avviṉāvē cey eṉa uṭaṉpāṭṭup poruḷ tantu niṟkum.
ēvalorumai vikuti puṇarntu keṭuvatāyiṉ ēvaṟpaṉmaiyum vikuti puṇarntu keṭal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam keṭāmaiyiṉ, orumai vikuti maṭṭum puṇarntu keṭumeṉṟal poruntātu.
iṉṉōraṉṉa iṭaṅkaḷilellām vaṭamoḻiyiyal vēṟu teṉmoḻi yiyal vēṟu eṉpatait teṟṟeṉa vuṇartal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉam uṇarātārē.
ceyeṉ ēval viṉaippakāp patamē
eṉa veḷḷiṭaiyāy āciriyar kūṟiṉum ataṉaiyum uṇarātu ivvāṟu mayaṅkak kūṟuvar.
3.	toḻiṟpeyar vikuti
pāṭu eṉpatu oru toḻiṟ peyar vikuti yeṉṟum, kōṭpāṭu eṉpatu ataṟkutāraṇa meṉṟum māṇavarkkup pāṭamāyuḷḷa ilakkaṇaṅkaḷellām mayaṅkik kūṟāniṟkum.
kōḷ eṉpatu oru mutaṉilai tirinta toḻiṟpeyar. piṉpu paṭu eṉṉum tuṇaiviṉai cērntu atuvum mutaṉilai tirintu pāṭu eṉṟāyiṟṟu. pāṭu eṉpatu vikutiyāyiṉ kōḷ eṉpatu pakuti yātal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamākāmaiyiṉ aḵturai yaṉmai yaṟika. puṟappaṭu, kaṭappaṭu eṉpavai puṟappāṭu kaṭappāṭu eṉṟā ṉāṟpōlak kōṭpaṭu eṉpatum kōṭpāṭu eṉṟāyiṟṟu.
vāypāṭu eṉpatilō veṉiṉ atilum paṭu eṉṉum tuṇai viṉaiyē mutaṉilai tirintatātal vēṇṭum.
4.	eḻuvāyurupu
avaṟṟuḷ,
eḻuvā yurupu tiripil peyarē
eṉṉum cūttira vuraiyil, tamakkeṉa vārāycciyiṉṟic cuvāmināta tēcikar pōṉṟa piṟar kūṟṟukaḷaiyē koṇṭu kūṟum orucār āciriyar.
eḻuvāy vēṟṟumaikku ai mutalāṉavai pōṉṟa urupu illātirun tālum, uraiyiṟkōṭal eṉkiṟa uttiyāl āṉavaṉ, ākiṉṟavaṉ, āvāṉ; eṉṟavaṉ, eṉkiṉṟavaṉ, eṉpavaṉ; avaṉ mutalāka aimpālilum varukiṟa col lurupukaḷ uṇṭeṉak koḷka  eṉṟu vaḻuppaṭavuraittaṉar. avar tamiḻilakkaṇattaiyē aṟiyār.
eḻuvāykku iyaṟkai vaṭivaṉṟi vēṟurupillai yeṉṟu tolkāppiyamum naṉṉūlum tulaṅkak kūṟum.
āṉavaṉ mutaliya mūṉṟum, eṉṟavaṉ mutaliya mūṉṟum muṟaiyē ā (āku), eṉ eṉṟa viṉaippakutikaḷiṉiṉṟum piṟanta viṉaiyā laṇaiyum peyarkaḷ. vēṟṟumai urupellām peyarkku īṟāyaṉṟit tamittu vārā. iṭaiccollātaliṉ tamittu varuvatum peyarccollāyiruppatum, aimpāl kāṭṭuvatum avaṟṟukku ilakkaṇa mākā. oru peyarkku maṟṟoru peyar urupāy varumeṉṟal eṭṭuṇaiyum poruntāta toṉṟām.
koṇṭu, nimittam mutaliya viṉaiyum peyarum piṟa vēṟṟumai kaḷukkuc collurupāy varumē yeṉiṉ, avaiyum avvav vēṟṟumaip poruḷpaṭa varumēyaṉṟi avaitāṉē vēṟṟumai yurupaṉmaiyum, iṭaiccol lākāmaiyum aṟintu koḷka.
āṉavaṉ mutaliya āṟu peyarkaḷum, cāttaṉ, cātti eṉṟa peyarkaḷaip pōlavē āṉavaṉai, āṉavaṉāl, āṉavaṉukku eṉa eṇ vēṟṟumaippaṭutaliṉ, avai peyarkaḷēyaṉṟi vēṟṟumaiyurupākāmai yaṟika. peyarukku vēṟṟumai yaṉṟi vēṟṟumaiyurupiṟku vēṟṟumai yiṉṟu.
cāttaṉ, cātti mutaliya peyarkaḷ mikak kuṟuki yiruttaliṉ, kaṭṭurai vakaiyāṉ ōcai nīṭaṟporuṭṭu cāttaṉāṉavaṉ, cāttiyāṉavaḷ eṉṟu cilarāṟ kūṟappaṭuvataṉṟip piṟa vēṟṟumai yurupukaḷaip pōla evviṭattum ellārāṉum kūṟappaṭuva talla.
ākavē mutal vēṟṟumaikku urupuṇṭeṉpatu pōliyurai yeṉa maṟukka.

5.	eḻuvāyc col
eḻuvāy peyarccollākavē yirukkumeṉṟu itukāṟum ellāc ciṟṟilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷḷum kūṟappaṭṭatu. atu ārāycciyiṉṟi vaḻivaḻi vantatōr kūṟṟām.
eḻuvāy nikaḻkāla viṉaiyeccamākavum irukkum.
e-ṭu : avaṉukkup pāṭat teriyum = avaṉukkup pāṭṭut teriyum.
pāṭa eṉpatu eḻuvāy.
āṅkilattil  infinitive mood (nikaḻkāla viṉaiyeccam) eḻuvāyāy varutalai oppunōkkuka. iṉṉōraṉṉa iṭaṅkaḷi lellām ottup pārvai ilakkaṇamē (Comparative Grammar) payaṉpaṭu meṉka.
iṉic ceyappaṭuporuḷum eḻuvāy pōla nikaḻkāla viṉaiyeccamā yirukkumeṉṟaṟika.
e-ṭu : avaṉ pāṭat terintu koṇṭāṉ = avaṉ pāṭṭait terintu koṇṭāṉ.
pāṭa ceyappaṭuporuḷ.
6.	uric col  (Poetic Diction)
uriccoṟ kiḷavi virikkuṅ kālai
........................................................
eccol lāyiṉum poruḷvēṟu kiḷattal   (tolcol 782)
eṉṉum tolkāppiyar cūttirattiṟkuc cēṉāvaraiyar
tamakkiyalpillā viṭaiccoṟ pōlātu icai kuṟippup paṇpeṉṉum poruṭkut tāmē yuriyavātaliṉ, uric col lāyiṟṟu. perumpāṉmaiyuñ ceyyuṭkuriyavāy varutaliṉ uriccollāyiṟṟeṉpāru muḷar.
icai kuṟippup paṇpeṉṉum poruḷavāyp peyar viṉai. pōṉṟum avaṟṟiṟku mutaṉilaiyāyun taṭumāṟi oru col poruṭkurittātalē yaṉṟi orucoṟ palaporuṭkum pala col oru poruṭkum uriyavāy varuvaṉa uriccolleṉ ṟum. avai peyarum viṉaiyum pōla īṟupaṟṟip poru ḷuṇartta lākāmaiyiṉ veḷippaṭāta vaṟṟai veḷippaṭṭa vaṟṟōṭu cārttit tammai yeṭuttōtiyē apporu ḷuṇarttap paṭumeṉṟum, uriccoṟkup potuvilakkaṇamum avaṟṟiṟkup poruḷuṇarttu muṟaimaiyu muṇarttiyavāṟu.
meytaṭumāṟalum orucoṟ pala poruṭ kurimaiyum palacol oruporuṭkurimaiyum uriccoṟku uṇmaiyāṉ ōtiṉā rēṉum, uriccoṟku ilakkaṇamāvatu icai kuṟippup paṇ peṉṉum poruṭkuriyavāy varutalē yām eṉṟu urai varaintār.
nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum ivvāṟē urai kūṟiṉār.
ellāc coṟkaḷam perumpālum icai kuṟippup paṇpu paṟṟiyē tōṉṟiyiruttaliṉ, icai kuṟippup  paṇpiṟkuriyaṉa uriccolleṉpatu poruntā; poruntumāyiṉ icai kuṟippup paṇpu paṟṟiya coṟkaḷellām uric collātal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamākāmaiyum, icaiyuṅ kuṟippum paṇpi laṭaṅkutalum, paṇpup peyar aṟuvakaip peyaruḷ oṉṟā talum, orucār toḻilum paṇpiṉpāṟpaṭṭut toḻiṟ paṇpeṉappaṭutalum, viṉaic coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālaṉa icaiyuṅ kuṟippum paṟṟi varutalum, ilakkaṇa muṇarntārk  kellām eḷitu pulaṉām.
meytaṭumāṟal coṟkaḷukkellām potuviyalpātaliṉ atuvum oru ciṟappilakkaṇamaṉṟu.
oru coṟ palaporuṭ kurimaiyum palacol oruporuṭ kurimaiyum peyar viṉai yiṭai yuri nāṉkiṟkum potuvāṉa tiricol ilakkaṇamātalai,
oru poruḷ kuṟitta vēṟucol lākiyum
vēṟuporuḷ kuṟitta oru col lākiyum
irupāṟ ṟeṉpa tiricoṟ kiḷavi   (tol. 882)
eṉpataṉāṟ kaṇṭukoḷka.
peyarkkum viṉaikkum urimai pūṇṭu niṟpaṉa uriccol leṉiṉ, iṭaiccollum aṅṅaṉam urimai pūṇṭu niṟṟaliṉ atuvum ilakkaṇamaṉmai yaṟika.
iṉi, uriccol ilakkaṇantāṉ eṉṉaiyō veṉiṟ kūṟutum:
colleṉap paṭupa peyarē viṉaiyeṉ
ṟāyiraṇ ṭeṉpa aṟintici ṉōrē   (tol. 643)
eṉpatāl ilakkaṇa vakaiyiṟ coṟkaḷ peyar viṉai eṉa iraṇṭēyeṉpatum, iṭaiccollāvaṉa ap peyariṭai yum viṉaiyiṭaiyum varum uṟuppukaḷeṉpatum, avvuṟuppu kaḷiṉṟip peyarp pakupataṅkaḷum viṉaippakupataṅkaḷumillai yeṉpatum, taṉittuvarum iṭaiccoṟ kaḷellām peyar viṉai yoṉṟaṉuḷ aṭaṅku meṉpatum, uriccol leṉpatōr ilakkaṇavakaic collaṉ ṟeṉpatum, atu peyarākavum viṉaiyākavu mirukkumeṉpatum, ceyyuṭkuriya tiricoṟkaṭ kellām poruḷuṇarttuvatē uriyiyaleṉpa tum, ceyyuṭkuriya coṟkaḷē uriccol leṉappaṭṭaṉa veṉpatum aṟiyappaṭum.
naṉṉūlārum.
palvakaip paṇpum pakarpeya rāki
orukuṇam palakuṇan taḻuvip peyarviṉai
oruvā ceyyuṭ kuriyaṉa vuriccol  (naṉ.442)
eṉat teḷḷitiṟ ṟerittār.
atuvumaṉṟi,
iṉṉa tiṉṉuḻi iṉṉaṇa miyalum
eṉṟicai nūluṭ kuṇakuṇip peyarkaḷ
collām parattaliṟ piṅkala mutalā
nallō ruriccolil nayantaṉar koḷalē.  (naṉ. 460)
eṉavum puṟaṉaṭai yōtiṉār.
itil piṅkala mutaliya nikaṇṭukaḷai yellām uriccol leṉṟamaiyum, avai ceyyuṭkuriya palavakai yaruñcoṟ kaḷukkum poruḷ kūṟutalaiyum, avaṟṟuḷ kāṅkēyar nikaṇṭu uriccolleṉṟē peyar peṟṟamaiyum kaṇṭukoḷka. tamiḻaṟivum tiricol vaḻakkum mikkirunta kālattil ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷellām iyaṟcoṟpōl eḷitāyp poruḷ viḷakkuvaṉavāyiṉa. piṉpu avai kuṉṟiya kālattu avaṟṟait taṉittaṉi akarātiyiyalpil eṭuttōtip poruḷkūṟa vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu.
peyarum viṉaiyumpōla īṟupaṟṟip poruḷuṇartta lākāmaiyiṉ, tammai yeṭuttōtiyē ap poruḷuṇarttap paṭutalāṉum eṉṟār cēṉāvaraiyarum. ovvorucollai yum taṉittaṉiyeṭuttōtip poruḷ kūṟuvateṉṟāl, atu ceyyuṭ collāy ulaka vaḻakki lillātateṉpatu eḷitiṟ pulaṉām, uriccoṟkaṭkellām poruḷ kūṟuvatait tolkāppiya uriyiyaluṭ kaṇṭukoḷka. uriyiyaleṉpatē ceyyuṭkuriya aruñcoṟkaṭ kellām poruḷuṇarttum akarātit toṭakkam. piṉpu atu virivāṉa nūlāy nīṇṭu nikaṇṭu eṉappaṭṭatu. nikaḷ-nīḷ. akalam nikaḷam eṉpatōr paṇṭai vaḻakku. nikaḷam - caṅkili;  nīḷamāyiruppatu. nikaḷam nīḷameṉa maruviṟṟu.
poruṭkup poruṭeriyi ṉatuvaram piṉṟē  (tol. col. 874)
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā  (tol.col. 877)
eṉat tolkāppiyar uriyiyaluṭ potuppaṭak kūṟuvatum itaṉai valiyuṟuttum.
peyarum viṉaiyumē mutaṉmutaluṇṭāṉa coṟkaḷ. avaṟṟiṉiṉṟum piṟkālattu iṭaiccol vakukkappaṭṭatu. ataṟkum piṉpu uriccoṟ pākupāṭuṇṭāyiṟṟu.



7. iṭukuṟip peyar
a,ā eṉṟaṟ ṟoṭakkattaṉa, iṭukuṟic ciṟappup peyar  eṉpatu naṉṉūṟ paḻaiyavurai.
a, ā eṉpavai eḻuttukaḷē yaṉṟi avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷākā. eḻuttāvatu oli, oru poruḷ vēṟu, ataṉ peyar vēṟu, kuṭam eṉpatu oru poruḷ, apporuḷ vēṟu; ataik kuṟikkum kuṭam eṉṉum col vēṟu. a, ā eṉpavai eḻuttukaḷākiya poruḷkaḷ, uyir, kuṟil, neṭil eṉpaṉavē avaṟṟiṉ peyarkaḷ, oru poruḷ peyariṉṟiyum yātāṉu mōr pulaṉākac cuṭṭappaṭum. a,ā eṉpavai peyarkaḷāyiṉ avaṟṟāṟ kuṟikkappaṭum poruḷkaḷ avaiyaṉṟi vēṟātal vēṇṭum. aṅṅaṉamiṉmaiyiṉ aḵturai yaṉmai yaṟika.
aṉṟiyum iṭukuṟiyeṉṟōr peyar yāṇṭumilatu.
ellāc collum poruḷkuṟit taṉavē
moḻipporuṭ kāraṇam viḻippat tōṉṟā
eṉṟār āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār.
aṟṟēl, makkaṭkiṭum iyaṟpeyarkaḷōveṉiṉ, avaiyum mutalāvatu iṭappaṭumpōtu kāraṇap peyarkaḷākavē yiruntiruttal vēṇṭum. (e-ṭu: murukaṉ = iḷaiñaṉ.  Victor -  veṟṟi peṟṟavaṉ) piṉṉarum avai, teyvappaṟṟu, atikāri māṭṭaṉpu. iṉamuṟai, iṉṉōcai, viḻumiya poruḷ, uyarnōkkam eṉpavaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu paṟṟiyē makkaṭkiṭappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
makkaṭkiṭappaṭum iyaṟpeyarellām mutalāvatu poruḷ kuṟittaṉaveṉṟum, piṉpu makkaṭpaṉmaiyāl makavu tōṟum putup peyariṭutalarumai paṟṟip paḻampeyarkaḷē mīṇṭum mīṇṭum piṟarkkiṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum peyiṉ (Bain) eṉṉum āṅkilap pulavar tam ilakkaṇattiṟ kūṟiyuḷḷār.
iṭukuṟippeya ruṇṭeṉṉum iḻukkuṇarcciyē cilar tavaṟ ṟārāyccikkum kāraṇamāyiruntu varukiṉṟatu.
tāvaraṅkaḷuḷ iṭukuṟip peyaruṭaiyavaiyellām tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkuriya veṉṟum, kāraṇap peyaruṭaiyavai yellām ayalnāṭṭiṟkuriyaveṉṟum, veṟṟilai (veṟumai +  ilai) miḷakāy (miḷaku + kāy) mutaliyavai kāraṇap peyaruṭaimai yāl ayalnāṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantavaiyeṉṟum, vāḻai, vēmpu mutaliyavai iṭukuṟip peyaruṭaimaiyāl tamiḻnāṭṭiṟkuriya veṉṟum, iyaṟkaip poruṭ kaṭṭurai  eṉṉum nūliṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ovvoru poruḷukkum taṉimoḻiyāyum toṭar moḻiyā yum oṉṟum palavum peyarirukkumeṉṟum. coṟkaḷ tōṉṟi ūḻikkaṇakkāṉa kālaṅkaḷākiyiruttaliṉ avaṟṟuṭ palavaṟ ṟiṉ poruḷkaḷ iṟantum maṟaintum irukkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, veṟṟilaikku aṭai eṉṟu maṟupeyar paṇṭai nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷateṉṟum, avaraiyiṉattaic cērnta kāy kottavarai yeṉṉappaṭṭāṟpōl, miḷakiṉattaic cērnta kāy miḷakā yeṉṉappaṭṭateṉṟum, vaḻavaḻa veṉṟiruppatu vāḻaiyeṉṟum, vemmaiyāṉa kālattil taḻaippatu vēmpu eṉṟum annūlāciriyar aṟintilar pōlum.
ikkālattiṟ cilar āṅkilamuṇarnta māttiraiyāṉē tamiḻu muṇarntōmeṉṟu tamiḻārāyat toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉar. atu nālvar kaṇṇilār nālvāy kaṇṭa tokkum.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟai oṉṟiṉa muṭittal taṉṉiṉa muṭittal eṉṉum uttiyāṟ koḷka.

8. pōli (naṉ. 122, urai)
mutaṟpōli iṭaippōlikaḷiṉpiṉ muṟaiyē colla vēṇṭiya ivviṟutip pōliyai muṉṉē coṉṉataṉāl. karumpu curumpar, vaṇṭu - vaṇṭar, ciṟaku - ciṟakar, iṭakku - iṭakkar eṉac cilar kuṟṟiyalukarap peyariṉiṟuti ukarattukku ar eṉpatu pōliyāka varutalum.... koḷka  eṉpatu.
kaṭaip pōliyiṉ ciṟappup paṟṟiyum ataṉ peruvaḻakkup paṟṟiyumē atu mutaliṟ kūṟappaṭṭatu. atu orukāl oṉṟaik kuṟippiṉum. pantal - pantar, kuṭal - kuṭar pōṉṟa pōlikaḷē koḷḷappaṭumallatu vaṇṭu - vaṇṭar, ciṟaku - ciṟakar eṉpavai koḷḷappaṭā.
kuṟṟiyalukaraṅkaḷ mikak kuṟukiya ōcaiyuṭaiyaṉavāyc colluk kīṟṟiliruppatu uccarippiṟ keḷitāyirāmai paṟṟi avaṟṟai (nīṭṭi) eḷitākkuvataṟku am, al, ar, i mutaliya cāriyaikaḷ varuvatuṇṭu. avai pōliyākā. ar eṉpatu pōliyāyiṉ ēṉaiyavum pōliyātal vēṇṭum. avai cāriyaiyātaliṉ aḵtu pōliyuraiyeṉa maṟukka.
e-ku: 	kuṉṟu - kuṉṟam	cukku - cukkal
	neñcu - neñcam	kuccu - kucci
	iṭakku - iṭakkar	kuñcu - kuñci
ar eṉpatu cāriyaiyāka nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭavillai. āyiṉum, atu cāriyai yeṉṟē koḷḷaṟpāṟṟu. al eṉpataṉ īṟṟup pōli eṉiṉum amaiyum.

piccu, vaicca eṉpavai ceyyuḷ vikāramēyaṉṟip pōliyākā, avai pōliyāyiṉ koccai  (Barbarism)  yeṉpatoṉ ṟiṉṟik kallāmākkaḷ pēcuvatellām pōliyuḷaṭaṅku meṉṟum.

9. pataviyal
naṉṉūṟ pataviya luraimukattil, iv viyalukku ātāram perumpālum vaṭamoḻiyātalāl atu tōṉṟa moḻi yiyal eṉap peyar kuṟiyāmal pataviyal eṉap peyar kuṟittār pōlum  eṉṟu āciriyar karuttai oruvāṟu ūṭuruvi yaṟin tāṟpōṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tolkāppiyattiṟ pataviya leṉṟōr iyalil pakupata uṟuppukaḷellām tokuttuk kūṟappaṭāviṭiṉum eḻuttati kāram collatikārameṉṉu mīratikāraṅkaḷilum āṅkāṅku vēṟuvēṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
pakutiyum vikutiyum peyariyal viṉaiyiyal iṭaiyiyalkaḷuḷ, cāriyaiyum vikāramum puṇariyalkaḷuḷḷum, iṭainilai yoṉṟum puṇariyal iṭaiyiyalkaḷuḷḷum tolkāppiyat tiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷamai kāṇka.
tolkāppiyak kālattu makkaḷ nuṇṇaṟiviṉarātaliṉ, avarkkup palaviyalkaḷuḷḷum vēṟu vēṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷa pakupata vuṟuppukaḷai mīṇṭumōriyalil tokuttuk kūṟa vēṇṭātāyiṟṟu. naṉṉūṟ kālattil makkaḷaṟivu kuṟain tamaiyiṉ avaṟṟait tokuttuk kūṟavum vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭāta pakupata vilakkaṇattai vaṭa moḻi taḻuvip putitāyc ceytār pavaṇantiyāreṉiṉ, avar kālam varaiyum tamiḻāṉatu āṅkilam pōlap pataviyaṉ moḻi yāyiruntila teṉavum, avaratu naṉṉūlālēyē vaṭamoḻi pōlap pataviyaṉ moḻiyāyiṟṟeṉavum iyaṟkaikkum moḻi nūlukkum muraṇpaṭumaṉṟō? ātalāl tolkāppiyar kālat tilēyē pakupata vilakkaṇa miruntateṉṟum, atu tol kāppiyattil āṅkāṅkuk kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷateṉṟum, atai maṟuttumōriyalil tokuttuk kūṟuvatu aṟṟai muṟaippaṭi kūṟiyatu kūṟaleṉṉum kuṟṟamākumeṉṟum uyttuṇarntu koḷka.

10.	ticaiccol
ticaic collāṉatu paṉṉiru koṭuntamiḻ nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum vantu centamiḻil vaḻaṅkum koṭuntamiḻc coṟkaḷēyaṉṟip piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷākā.
centamiḻ cērnta paṉṉiru nilattum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticaiccoṟ kiḷavi  (tol. col. 883)
eṉṟār āciriyar tolkāppiyaṉār. atai yuṇarātu,
centamiḻ nilañcēr paṉṉiru nilattiṉum
oṉpatiṟ ṟiraṇṭiṉil tamimoḻi nilattiṉum
taṅkuṟip piṉavē ticaiccoṟ kiḷavi  (naṉ. 273)
eṉṟu patiṉeṇmoḻi nilaṅkaḷaiyum kūṭṭiyuraittār pavaṇantiyār.
cēṉāvaraiyar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar mutaliya uraiyāciriya rellām koṭuntamiḻc coṟkaḷaiyē ticaiccoṟku utāraṇaṅ kāṭṭiṉar. teluṅku, kaṉṉaṭam, malaiyāḷam mutaliya moḻikaḷellām paṇṭaik koṭuntamiḻkaḷātaliṉ ticaic coṟ kutāraṇaṅ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. vaṭamoḻi yoṉṟē tolkāppiyar kālattu intu tēcattil vaḻaṅkiṉa piṟamoḻiyākum. atu ticaiccolliṉiṉṟum pirikkappaṭṭu vaṭacolleṉa vitantu kūṟappaṭṭatu. itaṉāl koṭuntamiḻc coṟkaḷē ticaic coṟkaḷeṉṟum piṟa moḻikaḷellām vaṭacoṟpōla avvam moḻip peyarālēyē kuṟikkappaṭu meṉṟum, avai ticaic colluḷaṭaṅkāveṉṟum peṟappaṭu māṟu kāṇka, iṅkilīcu. intuttāṉi, pōrttukkī mutaliya piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷaiyellām ticaic collāka ivaṟṟaic ciṟṟilakkaṇaṅkaḷ kūṟāniṟkum. avaṟṟai āṅkilac col, intuttāṉiccol, pōrttukkīciyac col eṉa avvam moḻip peyarālēyē kuṟittal vēṇṭum. ivai akkālat tiṉmaiyiṉ tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭila. piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷaiyellām tārāḷamāyt tamiḻil vaḻaṅkumāṟu ticaic collāk koḷḷa ilakkaṇikaḷ iṭan tantārāyiṉ. ērāḷamāṉa piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷ yātum varampiṉṟit tamiḻiṟ kalantu ataṉ iṉimaiyaiyum taṉimaiyaiyum kulaittuviṭutal tiṇṇam. atōṭu avai tamiḻukku iṉṟiyamaiyātavai yeṉṟum, avaiyiṉṟit tamiḻil muṟṟap poruḷaṟivuṟuttalamaiyāteṉṟum, eṇṇap paṭum, ātaliṉ ilakkaṇikaḷ iṭantantilar. iṅkilīcu pōṉṟa moḻikaṭkē ticaic coṟkaḷ iṉṟiyamaiyātavaiyākum. tamiḻilirunta ettuṇaiyō coṟkaḷ vaḻakkiṉṟi iṟan toḻintaṉa. āyiṉum iṟṟaik kālattum, piṟacoṟ kalavātu karuttellāvaṟṟaiyum uṇarttavallatu tamiḻ oṉṟēyākum. āṅkilattai vaṭamoḻi yutaviyiṉṟit tamiḻil moḻi peyarkka muṭiyāteṉṟu cilar karutukiṉṟaṉar. mūlappakuti kaḷiṉiṉṟum iyaṟcoṟkaḷiṉiṉṟum tiricoṟkaḷum toṭarccoṟkaḷum ākkumāṟu avaraṟintārāyiṉ aṅṅaṉam karutār. ovvoru moḻiyiṉum piṟamoḻiyaic cevvaiyāy moḻi peyarppatu aritākum. āyiṉum, tamiḻil ēṉaiyavaṟ ṟiṟpōl attuṇai aritaṉṟu, itupōtu nūl vaḻakkiṉum ulaka vaḻakkilumuḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷaiyē tamiḻ makkaḷ aṟintilar.
tamiḻukku vaṭamoḻi yutavi vēṇṭātirukka, vaṭacollai yēṉ tamiḻilakkaṇaṅ kūṟa vēṇṭumeṉiṉ, vaṭamoḻi tamiḻukku iṭattāl aṇittātaliṉ pōkkuvaravupaṟṟiyum moḻi peyarppup paṟṟiyum vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa nūṟpeyar kaḷum āṭpeyarkaḷumāyt tamiḻil vantu vaḻaṅkiya ciṟappup peyarkaḷē vaṭacolleṉap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟum, moḻi vaḻak kiṟkum karuttaṟivippiṟkum vēṇṭiya piṟa potuc coṟkaḷ tamiḻil vantu vaḻaṅkumāṟu kūṟappaṭṭila veṉṟum paṇṭai nūlkaḷellām vaṭamoḻi viravāta centamiḻ nūlkaḷeṉṟum kūṟiviṭukka.

eṉavē, centamiḻ nāṭṭai nāṟṟicaiyum cūḻnta paṉṉiru koṭun tamiḻnāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vantu vaḻaṅkum koṭuntamiḻc coṟkaḷē ticaic coṟkaḷeṉṟum, piṟamoḻic coṟkaḷ tamiḻukku  vēṇṭuva vallaveṉṟum, orukāl vēṇṭiṉum, vaṭacoṟpōla avvam moḻiyāṟ kuṟikkappaṭumeṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.

cāttaṉ varutaṟku muriyaṉ eṉpatu varāmaikku muriyaṉeṉ patait taḻuvi niṟṟaliṉ, atu ēṉaiyatu taḻīiya eccavummai yaṉṟi etirmaṟaiyummai yākātu. atu piṟitoru poruḷait taḻuvāviṭiṉum piṟatoruviṉaiyait taḻuvi niṟkumāṟaṟika. cāttaṉ varutaṟku muriyaṉ eṉpatu etirmaṟai yummaiyāyiṉ, cāttaṉ varāmaikku muriyaṉ eṉpatu uṭaṉpāṭṭummaiyātal vēṇṭum. uṭaṉpāṭṭai oḻipāyuṭaittāy  niṟṟaliṉ, avvāṟu uṭaṉpāṭṭummai cūttirattiṟ kūṟappaṭāmaiyiṉ aḵturaiyaṉmai yaṟika. piṉṉai yātōtāṉ etirmaṟaiyummai yeṉiṉ; nāḷ tavaṟiṉum nāttavaṟāṉ eṉpuḻi, nāḷ; tavaṟiṉum eṉpatu tavaṟātu eṉṉum oḻipai uṭaittāy niṟṟaliṉ atuvē etirmaṟaiyummaiyeṉka. kaṭal vaṟṟiṉuṅ kaṅkai vaṟṟātu. teyvam tīmai ceyyiṉum koṟṟaṉ tīmai ceyyāṉ eṉpaṉavum ivai pōṉṟa piṟavumataṟkutāraṇam. e-ṭu:	iraṇṭēkāl - (eṇṇal)

nilamē nīrē tīyē
iyai eṉṉum colliṉ tiripāṉa ēy eṉṉum coṟkup poruntal allatu kūṭutaṟ poruḷuṇmaiyāl, ataṉ kaṭaikkuṟaiyāṉa ēkāra viṭaiccol, eṇṇup poruḷil vantateṉa vaṟika.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavaṟṟāl, iḷ eṉṉum vērccolliṉ aṭippaṭaip poruḷ poruntutal eṉpatu uṇarappaṭum.
iḷam payirvakai 
nāṟṟu nel kattari mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ iḷanilai; 
kaṉṟu mā puḷi vāḻai mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ iḷanilai:
karuntu vāḻaiyiṉ iḷanilai;
piḷḷai teṉṉaiyiṉ iḷanilai;
kuṭṭi viḷāviṉ iḷanilai;
maṭal allatu vaṭali paṉaiyiṉ iḷanilai:
paiṅkūḻ nel cōḷam mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pacum payir.   (col : 71)
iḷavaṭṭak kācu
tirumaṇattaṉṟu maṇamakkaḷ ūrvalam varuvatum, cila ciṟṟūr kaḷil ayalūr maṇamakaṉ vantu oru peṇṇai maṇantu mīḷumpōtu, avvūr iḷaiñar iḷavaṭṭakkācu eṉṉum kainīṭṭam kēṭpatum, paṇṭaik kālattil oru peṇṇai nōkkip pala iḷaiñar pōṭṭiyiṭṭup porutataik kuṟippāy uṇarttum.  (ta.ti : 5)
iṟakup peyar
tūvu allatu tūvi paṟavaiyiṉ uṭampiluḷḷa ciṟu tūvu; iṟaku ciṟakilum vālilumuḷḷa peruntūvu.  (col : 52.)
iṟappu 
makkaḷ piṟappu kaṇviḻippum, avaratu iṟappu kaṇṇaṭaippum pōṉṟatu, vāḻkkai muḻuvatum oru pakal naṭavaṭikkai pōṉṟatē. peṟṟōr tam piḷḷaikaḷait tam kaṇṇuḷḷapōtē karaiyēṟṟa vēṇṭum eṉṟu colvatu vaḻakkam. pakal muṭintu iravu vanta piṉ kaṇ mūṭit tūṅkuvatu pōl vāḻkkai muṭinta pōtum makkaḷ tam kaṇ mūṭi viḻiyāt tuyil koḷvar.
uṟaṅkuvatu pōlum cākkā ṭuṟaṅki
viḻippatu pōlum piṟappu  (kuṟaḷ 239)
tuñciṉār cettāriṉ vēṟallar (kuṟaḷ 926)
eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar  (col. 9)
iṟai 
iṟai allatu iṟaivaṉ eṉpatu, aracaṉukkum kaṭavuṭkum potup peyar. kaṭavuḷ eṅkum taṅkiyiruppatu pōla araca ṉuṭaiya āṇai, avaṉuṭaiya nāṭeṅkum taṅkiyirukkiṟatu eṉpatu karuttu. iṟuttal - taṅkutal. col.24.
iṟaivaṉ tāḷ - tamiḻaka maruntukaḷ perumpālum taḻaiyum taṇṭumāy iruttaliṉ piṟavippiṇikku maruntākum kuṟippup paṭa iṟaivaṉ tiruvaṭikaḷ naṟṟāḷām.  (ti.ma.37)
iṉpam 
nāṭaka naṭippu, aḻakiya iyaṟkaik kāṭci, tiruviḻā, icai yaraṅku, coṟpoḻivu, nālvakai aṟucuvai uṇṭi, naṉkāṟṟu, iṉiya illaṟa vāḻvu, taṭpaveppac camanilai, nilai viḷai yāṭṭu, nīr viḷaiyāṭṭu, vēlaivāyppu, varippaḷu iṉmai, ceṅkōlāṭci mutaliyaṉa.  (kuṟaḷ 738)
iṉpattuppāliṉ irutiṟam
cila āriya nūlkaḷaik kaṟṟa tuṇaiyāṉē tammaic ciṟanta aṟiñarākak karutik koḷḷum maṭattalaivarum pōlittuṟavi yarum, tirukkuṟaḷ iṉpattuppālaik koṇṭiruppatāl tuṟaviyar kaṟkat takka nūlaṉṟeṉṟu tuṇintu kūṟuvar.  iṟaiva ṉukkum ātaṉukku miṭaippaṭṭa aṉpai uvamai vāyilāka viḷakkutaṟku, kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyariṭaippaṭṭa kātaliṉuñ ciṟantatu vēṟiṉmai yāṉēyē, civaṉaṭiyāruṭ ciṟanta māṇikka vācakar ciṟṟampalak kōvaiyaip pāṭiṉāreṉṟum, ataṉ veḷipporuḷ ulakaneṟiyum uṭporuḷ vīṭṭuneṟiyum paṟṟiṉaveṉṟum, aṟintu koḷka.
āraṇaṅkā ṇeṉpa rantaṇar yōkiyar ākamattiṉ
kāraṇaṅkā ṇeṉpar kāmukar kāmanaṉ ṉūlateṉpar
ēraṇaṅkā ṇeṉpa reṇṇa reḻutteṉpa riṉpulavōr
cīraṇaṅ kākiya ciṟṟam palakkōvai ceppiṭiṉē.  (taṉiyaṉ)
ittakaiyatē tirukkuṟa ḷiṉpattup pālumeṉcu.
iṉṉutal
iṉṉutal iṉittal allatu iṉpamātal.
iṉ-iṉpu-iṉpam . iṉ-iṉi. iṉṉā, iṉiya   eṉpataṉ etir maṟaiyāṉa palaviṉpāl viṉaiyālaṇaiyum peyar.  (ti.ma.178)
īkārac cuṭṭu
aṇmai, piṉmai, iḻuttal
1.	aṇmai
muṉmaiyaik kuṟittaṟku muṉṉōkkik kuvinta vāyitaḻ, muṉmaikkup piṟpaṭṭa aṇmaiyaik kuṟittataṟkup piṉ ṉōkku mukattāl īkārattait tōṟṟuvittatu. atu aṇmaic cuṭṭāyiṟṟu.
aṇmaic cuṭṭu
peyar : ītu - itu.
	īṉ = ivviṭam, ivvulakam; īṉōr = ivar,
	ivvulakattār.
	il - iḵtu - ittu
	iv - iva - ivai.
	ivaṉ, ivaḷ, ivar.
	ikam = ivvulakam, ivviṭam, ñālam.  (mu.tā)
peyareccam: ī - i.
		īṉ - iṉ - iṉṉa.
		īṉ - īṉa - inta.
viṉaiyeccam:	īṅku - iṅku - iṅkā - iṅkai.
		īṅku - īṅkaṇ - īṅkaṉ - īṅkaṉam
		īṅkaṉ-iṅkaṉ-iṅṅaṉam
		iṅkaṉ - iṅṅaṉ - iṅṅaṉam
		īṇṭu: 
ivaṇ.
im - impu - impar.
viṉaiyeccamum
iṭaiccollum :	iṉ - iṉṟu. iṉ - iṉṉē:
		iṉ - iṉṉum = ituvaraiyum:
		iṉ - iṉi. iṉṉiṉi (aṭukku):
		iṉ - iṉṉā
		ita - itā - itō - itōḷ - 
		itōḷi.  (mu.tā)
		intā - intō.
2. piṉmai
muṉmaikkup piṉmaiyāṉa aṇmaiyaik kuṟittataṟku vāyitaḻ piṟpaṭuvatāl, īkārac cuṭṭu aṇmaikkaṭuttup piṉmai yuṇarttiṟṟu.
1.	piṉmai (kālamum iṭamum)
	iṉ - iṉi = iṉimēl, piṉpu.
	iṉ - iṉṉum = itaṟku mēlum.
	piṉ - piṉṉam - piṉṉar. piṉ- piṉṉē.
	piṉ - piṉpu. piṉ-piṉṟu- pintu. piṉ-piṟku
	piṉ-piṟpāṭu (piṉ+pāṭu).
	piṉ - piṟa- piṟavu - piṟaku - piṟakku. piṟakkiṭutal= piṉṉukkuc cellutal.
piṟakku - piṟaṅku - piṟaṅkaṭai = piṉṉāl varum urimaiyāḷaṉ (vāricu).
piṉ - piṉam - piṟam - piṟampu:  piṟampattaṅkāl - piṉṉaṅkāl.
piṟam - piṭam - piṭar - piṭari = talaiyiṉ piṉpuṟam.
piṭam - piṭṭam = piṉpuṟam, piṉpuṟattiṉ kaṭai allatu aṭippakuti (kuṇṭi).
piṭṭam - puṭṭam, piṭṭam - piṭṭi-puṭṭi= paṟavaiyiṉ piṉpuṟam. 
piṟam - puṟam - puṟaṉ. puṟakkaṭai = vīṭṭiṉ piṉ pakkam.
puṟaṅkūṟutal = piṉṉāṟ paḻittal, puṟaṉ - (puraṉi) - puṟaṇi = puṟaṅkūṟṟu.
puṟaṉē = piṉpu, pinti : puṟaṉaṇṭai = piṉpuṟam. piṟaku - puṟaku = piṉpu.
II  maṟunilai
iṉ - iṉṉum = tirumpavum, mēlum, vēṟum:
iṉṉoṉṟu = vēṟoṉṟu. iṉṉoru = vēṟoru, maṟṟa.
iṉ - ēṉ. ēṉōr = maṟṟōr. ēṉ- ēṉai= maṟṟa.
piṉ = tirumpa, vēṟu. piṉṉum = tirumpavum.
piṉṉum piṉṉum = tirumpat tirumpa.
piṉṉoṉṟu = vēṟoṉṟu. piṉṉē yār? = vēṟu yār?
piṉa - piṟa- piṟitu. piṟa- piṟaku= vēṟu. piṟaku- piṟakku = vēṟāka.
piṟam - piṟaṉ + vēṟoruvaṉ, maṟṟavaṉ, ayalāṉ, 
piṟam - puṟam - puṟampu = maṟṟai.
III  mutuku 
mutuku piṉpuṟamiruppatāl piṉmaip peyar mutukaik kuṟittaṟkāyiṟṟu.
piṉ- piṟku. piṟam- piṟavu-piṟaku= mutuku. =piṟaku - piṟakku = mutuku.
piṟaku - puṟaku. piṟakku - puṟakku. puṟakkiṭutal= puṟaṅkāṭṭutal.
piṟam - puṟam - puṟaṉ, puṟam =mutuku. puṟam- puṟampu = mutuku.
piṟam- piṟaṉ- verinta- ven-veṉ=mutuku.
piṟam- piṭam-piṭṭam- mutuku.
IV  mēṟpuṟam
vilaṅkukaṭkum paṟavaikaṭkum kuppuṟappaṭuttirukkum māntaṉuk kum mutuku mēṟpuṟamāyiruppatāl, mutukiṉ peyar mēṟ puṟattaik kuṟittatu.
puṟam= mēṟpakkam, pakkam.
puṟam- puṟaṉ-(puṟaṉi)-puṟaṇi= mēṟpuṟam.
V  veḷippuṟam
maṭikkappaṭum allatu karuṭṭappaṭum poruḷkaṭku muṉ pakkam uṭpuṟamum piṉpakkam veḷippuṟamum pōṉṟirutta lālum, uyiraṟṟa kaṉapporuḷkaṭku mēṟpuṟam muḻutum veḷippuṟamāyiruntalālum, mēṟpuṟattaik kuṟikkum puṟam eṉṉum col veḷippuṟattaiyuṅ kuṟittatu:
puṟam= veḷi, veḷippakkam, puṟap poruḷ.
puṟam- puṟavu- puṟaku= veḷippuṟam, puṟampāṉavaṉ. puṟaku- puṟakku.
puṟam- puṟampu= veḷippuṟam.
puṟampu- puṟampar. puṟappaṭu- puṟappāṭu, puṟappaṭutal= veḷi varutal. 
uḷḷum puṟampum  akamum puṟamum eṉṉun toṭarkaḷāl, puṟam eṉṉum col uṭpakkattiṟku etirāṉa veḷip pakkattaik kuṟippatu teḷivām. mūṭiṉa kaiyiṉ uṭpuṟamā yiruppatu uḷḷaṅkai allatu akaṅkai eṉṟum, ataṉ veḷippuṟamāyiruppatu puṟaṅkai eṉṟum kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka.
puṟam = maruta nilattiṟkup puṟampāṉa mullai, mullaiyaṭutta kuṟiñci.
puṟam- puṟavu= mullai, kuṟiñci. puṟam- puṟampu- puṟampaṇai= mullai, kuṟiñci.
puṟam- puṟa= mullai niṟap paṟavai vakai, puṟa-puṟā- puṟavu- puṟavam.
piṭṭam- pīṭṭaṉ = puṟampāṉavaṉ. 
VI  piṉṉiṭal 
iḷ- iḷittal= vāyitaḻaip piṉṉukkiḻuttal. vāyait tiṟantu pallaik kāṭṭal.
iḷ-iri, irital= piṉṉukkōṭutal, tōṟṟōṭutal, ōṭutal,
iḷ-iṭa, iṭattal= ilai pū mutaliyavaṟṟaip piṉṉukkiḻut toṭittal.
iḷ-(iṇ)- iṇuṅku, iṇuṅkutal= tirukip piṉṉuk kiḻuttal, piṉṉukkiḻut toṭittal.
iṭa-iṭakku= piṟcelavu. kutirai iṭakkup paṇṇukiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakkaik kāṇka.
iṭa-iṭai. iṭaital= piṉvāṅkutal.
iṭai- iṭaiñcal = piṉvāṅkac ceyyum taṭai.
iṭa-iṭaṟu. iṭaṟutal= piṉṉukkut taḷḷutal.
iḻuttal
iyalpāka iḻuttaleṉpatu, muṉṉāluḷḷa poruḷaip piṉṉukku valintu koṇartalātaliṉ, piṉmaik karuttil iḻuttaṟ karuttut tōṉṟiṟṟu.
i  iḻuppu
iḷ- iḷai- iḷaippu. iḷaittal= mūccu vēkamāy iḻuttal.
iḷ-iḻu-iḻuppu, iḻu- iḻuvai.
iḻu-iḻai= iḻukkappaṭṭa nūl. iḻaittal= nūl iḻaittal, uḷiyai iḻuttut tēyttal, iḻai- iḻaippu, iḻaippuḷi= iḻaikkum uḷi.
iḷ- iru- īr. īrttal= iḻuttal, iḻuttaṟuttal.
īr- īruḷ= mūcciḻukkum uṟuppākiya īral.
īr- īral.
īr-īrkku= aṟukkum allatu aṟukkappaṭṭa cimpu. 
II  iḻuppunōy
iḷ- iḷai- iḷaippu. iḷai-īḷai= kācam.
iḷ-iḻu- iḻuppu. iḻu-icu-icivu= caṉṉi. 
icittal= narampiḻuttal.  (mu.tā.)
īkārac cuṭṭuk karuttu vaḷarcci
īkārac cuṭṭu, mutalāvatu aṇmaiyaik kuṟikkum. aṇmai muṉmaikkup piṉmaiyātalālum, īkārattaiyolikkum pōtu vāy piṉṉukkiḻuppatālum, aṇmaik kaṭutta īkārac cuṭṭuk karuttu piṉmaiyākum.
piṉmaik karuttil tōṉṟuvatu piṟpaṭaṟ karuttu.
māntaṉ niṉṟa nilaiyil iyaṟkaiyāy oṉṟai iḻuppatu muṉṉiṉṟu piṉṉātaliṉ, piṟpaṭaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu piṟpaṭuttaṟ karuttākiya iḻuttaṟ karuttup piṟakkum.
mēṉōkkic ceṉṟa poruḷ piṟpaṭutal iṟaṅkalātaliṉ, piṟpaṭaṟ karuttiṉiṉṟu iṟaṅkal allatu kīḻuṟaṟ karuttut tōṉṟum.
īkārac cuṭṭāluṇarttappeṟum peruṅ karuttukkaḷ, aṇmai piṉmai kīḻuṟal eṉṉum mūṉṟē. mutukiṟkup piṉ nikaḻvatu oruvarukkun teriyāmaiyāṉum, muṉṉōkkik koṇṭu piṉṉukkuc celvatu māntaṉukkum maṟṟavuyirikaṭkum. iyaṟkai yaṉmaiyāṉum, piṉmaik karuttil vēṟoṉṟun tōṉṟaṟ killai. mēṉiṉṟiṟaṅkum oru poruḷ nilattil allatu nīril viḻuntu ataṟkuḷ pukiṉum kīḻuṟal allatu iṟaṅkutalākiya aḷavē īkārac cuṭṭuk karuttellaikkuṭ paṭṭatām. oru poruḷukkuṭ pukuvatellām tuḷaittaliṉ pāṟpaṭṭatāka liṉ. atu ūkārac cuṭṭiṉ āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭateṉa aṟika.
ītal vakai
iṭattil vaṟiyārkku nilamum,
karuviyil vaṟiyārkkup poṟiyum,
nalattil vaṟiyārkku maruntum,
aṟivil vaṟiyārkku nūlum
kāppil vaṟiyārkkup pātukāppum
aḷippatum ītal vakaikaḷē   (ti.ma.138)
īntu
īntu hintāla
īrtal= aṟuttal. uyirīrum vāḷatu  (kuṟaḷ .334)
īr = nuṇmai. īrayir maruṅkiṉa (cilap.6: 146).
īr - īrntu- īntu= kiḷaiyillā maraṅkaṭkuḷ mikac ciṟiya ōlaiyuḷḷatu, allatu ēṟuvārai aṟukkum aṭiyiṉatu. ma. ītta, te. ītta.  (va.va : 90)
īyam
īyam - sīsa (vē)
iḷ - iḷ - iḷaku, iḷ - iy - ī - īyam = eḷitil, iḷakuvatu.  (va.va : 90)
īḻavar 
makkaḷ paṟpala camayaṅkaḷil tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉiṉṟu īḻattiṟkuk kuṭiyēṟiyatu pōṉṟē, īḻattiṉiṉṟum tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟkuk kuṭiyēṟi vantirukkiṉṟaṉar. karikāl vaḷavaṉ īḻattiṉ mēl paṭaiyeṭuttup paṉṉīrāyiram kuṭikaḷaic ciṟaippiṭittuk koṇṭu vantu kāvirikkuk karai kaṭṭuvittatāka avaṉ meyk kīrtti kūṟum. iṅṅaṉam koṇṭu varappaṭṭa varum kuṭiyēṟiya varumē īḻavar eṉappaṭṭaṉar. malaiyāḷa nāṭṭil ivarait tīvār allatu tīyār eṉpar.  (col. 27)
u  eṉṉum vērccol
(ukaraccuṭṭu)
muṉmaiccuṭṭu
u (muṉmaik karuttu vēr)
ukarattai olikkuṅkāl, itaḻ kuvintu muṉṉōkkuvatāl, ukaraccīṭṭu, mutaṟkaṇ, pēcuvōṉiṉ muṉṉiṭattaik kuṟittatu.
utukkāṇ = eṉmuṉ pār !
utā, utō
untā, untō = eṉ muṉṉē !
utōḷ utōḷi = eṉakku muṉṉiṭattil.
unta = eṉakku muṉṉuḷḷa.
uṅku, ūṅku = eṉakku muṉ.
uvaṉ = eṉakku muṉ niṟpavaṉ.
iṅṅaṉamē, uvaḷ, uvar, utu, uvai eṉa ēṉaip pālilum oṭṭuka.
ukarac cuṭṭu tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaḻakkaṟṟatu, āyiṉum, yāḻp pāṇattil vaḻaṅkuvatākat terikiṉṟatu. itu kuṭiyēṟṟap pātukāppu eṉṉum neṟimuṟaippaṭṭatu.

uṭkoḷḷum vakai
acaittal vilaṅku pōl acaiyiṭṭut tiṉṉutal, atukkutal cūṭāṉa uṇavai vāyiṉ irupuṟattilum māṟi māṟi otukkutal; arittal pūcci puḻuppōlac ciṟitu ciṟitāyk kaṭittal; aruntu tal ciṟitu ciṟitāyt tiṉṉutal allatu kuṭittal; ārtal vayiṟu nirampavuṇṇutal; uṇṇutal etaiyum uṭkoḷḷutal; utapputal (kutapputal) vāyiliruntu veḷivarumpaṭi mikutiyāyc cavaittal; uṟiñcutal oṉṟiluḷḷa nīrai vāyāl uḷḷiḻuttal; otukkutal oru kaṉṉattil aṭakkutal; kaṭittal kaṭiṉamāṉa taip pallāl uṭaittal; karumputal oru poruḷiṉ ōrattil ciṟitu ciṟitāyk kaṭittal; kaṟittal mellak kaṭittal; kuṭittal kalattiluḷḷa nīraip potuvakaiyil vāyiliṭṭu uṭkoḷḷu tal kutaṭṭutal kālnaṭaipōl acaiyiṭṭu vāykku veḷiyē taḷḷutal; koṟittal ovvoru kūlamaṇiyāyp palliṭai vaittu umiyaip pōkkutal; capputal cavaittu oṉṟaṉ cāṟṟai uṭkoḷḷutal;
cavaittal veṟṟilai, pukaiyilai mutaliyavaṟṟai mellutal; cāppiṭutal cōṟuṇṇutal; cuvaittal oṉṟaṉ cuvaiyai nukartal; cūpputal kaṭiṉamāṉataic capputal; tiṉṉutal meṉṟu uṭkoḷḷutal; nakkutal nāviṉāl toṭutal; parukutal kaiyiṉāl āvalōṭu aḷḷik kuṭittal; māntutal orē viṭukkil allatu perumaṭakkāyk kuṭittal; mukkutal allatu mokkutal vāy niṟaiya oṉṟaiyiṭṭut tiṉṉutal; mellutal pallāl araittal; mēytal mēlākap pullait tiṉṉutal; viḻuṅkutal mellāmalum palliṟ paṭāmalum viraintu uṭ koḷḷutal; micaittal miccil uṇṇutal.  (col : 55)
uṭpakai
akattārē taṉṉalam nōkkiyum kalām paṟṟiyum tam iṉat tāraip puṟattārāṉa pakaivarkkuk kāṭṭik koṭuttal.  (ti.mi. ati. 89)
uṭampu
uyirillāta poruḷkaḷ ettaṉaiyō vēṟupaṭṭa vaṇṇam vaṭivu aḷavu cuvai mutaliya taṉmaikaḷaiyuṭaiyavaṉāy iruntālum avaṟṟai ellām uṭampoṭu toṭarpu paṭutti oṉṟākak koṇṭu avaṟṟukku uṭampu allatu mey eṉa orē peyariṭṭataṟkuk kāraṇam aṇṭattai ottatu piṇṭam eṉṟum, uyiraṟṟa poruḷaḷaittum aimpūtac cērkkai allatu vēṟupāṭu eṉṟum muṉṉait tamiḻar aṟintiruntamaiyē.  (col.31.)
uṭal
uṭal, uṭampu, kuṭampai, kūṭu, mey, yākkai, muṭai, kaṭṭai eṉa uṭampiṟku vaḻaṅkum peyarkaḷiṉ kāraṇattai nōkkiṉ appeyarkaḷai iṭṭa makkaḷiṉ uyariya aṟivu pulaṉām.
uṭal -	uyiruṭaṉ cērntē iyaṅkuvatu allatu uyiruṭaṉ iruppatu, uṭal allatu uṭampu.
kūṭu -	kuñcu poritta piṉ toṭarpu nīṅkum muṭṭaik kūṭu pōla uyiraiviṭṭu nīṅkuvatu kuṭampai allatu kūṭu. (kuṟaḷ. 333)
mey - 	uyirai mēlākap potintippatu mey.
yākkai - tōl narampu elumpu tacai arattam mutaliya eḻuvakait tātukkaḷāl yākkappeṟṟiruppatu allatu  muṭaiyappaṭṭiruppatu yākkai allatu muṭai.
kaṭṭai -	uyirnīṅkiya piṉ kaṭṭai pōlakkiṭantu erivatu allatu maṇṇōṭu maṇṇāyp pōvatu kaṭṭai  (col. 31)
uṭal nalap poruttam
maṇamakkaḷ iṉpan tuyttaṟkum illaṟam naṭattaṟkum iṉṟiyamaiyātatu uṭal nalam. cila nōykaḷ maṟaivāyirukkum, allatu maṟaikkappaṭṭirukkum. ātalāl, takka maruttuvap paṇṭitaraik koṇṭu maṇamakkaḷai nōṭṭañ ceytal vēṇṭum. allākkāl oruvarāl iṉṉoruvar nōyppaṭuva tuṭaṉ, avarkkup piṟakkum piḷḷaikaḷum vaḻivaḻi nōyp paṭṭu varunta vēṇṭiyirukkum.  (ta.ti. 43,44.)
uṭal valiyāl tīmai
oruvarkku uṭal pōṉṟatē uḷḷamum valimaiyaṭaital vēṇṭum. uḷḷa valimaikku aṭippaṭai aṟivē. itaṉāl, aṟivai uraṉ eṉṟār tiruvaḷḷuvar. uraṉ-valimai.
uraṉeṉṉun tōṭṭiyāṉ ōraintuṅ kāppāṉ
 varaṉ eṉṉum vaippiṟkōṟ vittu   (kuṟaḷ-24)
eṉpatu kuṟaḷ.
aṟivē āṟṟal (Knowledge is power) eṉpar āṅkilarum.
uḷavalimaiyōṭu kūṭiya uṭalvalimai kūṭat tīmai viḷaikkum eṉpatu, tuṟaviyar karuttu. itaṉālēyē avar nōṉpālum tavattālum tam uṭalai oṭukkuvar. uḷavalimaiyōṭu kūṭāta uṭal valimaiyō. piṟarkkup peruntīṅku viḷaippatu tiṇṇam.
uṭal paruttavaṉaik kuṇṭaṉ eṉṟum taṭiyaṉ eṉṟum kūṟuvatu ulaka vaḻakku. ippeyarkaḷ tīyavaṉ allatu koṭiya vaṉ eṉṉum poruḷaip peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. tēvāra āciriyarum tivviyappaṉuval āciriyaruṅkūṭac camaṇaraik kuṇṭar eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
uṭal taṭittavaṉ kuṟumpu allatu tīṅku ceyvāṉ eṉpatu, mēṟkūṟiya vaṟṟāl viḷaṅkum.  (col: 111)
uṭaṟ peyar
uṭal - uṭampil kaikāl talai aṟṟa naṭuppakuti.
uṭampu - muḻu uṭampu (ivai āṭcip poruḷkaḷ)
uṭakku - uḷḷīṭu aṟṟa uṭampuk kūṭu
yākkai - eḻuvakait tātukkaḷāl kaṭṭappaṭṭatu.
mey - uyirai mēlākap potintiruppatu.
mēṉi - uṭampiṉ mēṟpuṟam.  (col. 40)
uṭaṉpōkku
uṭaṉ pōkkāvatu, kaḷavoḻukkam taṭaippaṭṭa vipattu, allatu kātaliyiṉ peṟṟōr avaḷait tara icaiyāviṭattu, kātalaṉ avaḷai vēṟṟūrkkēṉum taṉ vīṭṭiṟkēṉum aḻaittuk koṇṭu pōy viṭal.  (ta.ti. 6)
uṭukku
uṭukku-huṭukka
uṭu = oṭu. uṭukku = ōṭukku. ōṭu-oṭuku-oṭuṅku-ōṭukku-oṭukkam. uṭuku, uṭuṅku, oṭuku eṉpaṉa iṟantupaṭṭaṉa. uṭukku = iṭaiyoṭuṅkiya ciṟupaṟai. uṭukku-uṭukkai. vaṭamoḻiyil mūlamillai.  (va.va:91)
uṇṭip poruttam
maravuṇavum pulāluṇavum ākiya iraṇṭaṉuḷḷum oṉṟil ottiruttalum, ovvātu vēṟupaṭiṉ avvēṟu pāṭṭiṟku iṭantaralum uṇṭip poruttamām.  (ta.ti.44)
uṇṇam
uṇṇam-uṣṇa (i.vē.)
uḷ-uṇ-uṇmai = veppam.
uṇṇa vaṇṇat toḷinañcamuṇṭu   (tēvā.510:6).
uṇ = uṇa. uṇattal = kāytal, ulartal.
uṇ = uṇattu. uṇattutal = kāyappōṭutal, ularttutal.
talaiyai (muṭiyai) uṇattutal eṉpatu nellai nāṭṭu vaḻakku.
miḷakāy vaṟṟalai uṇanta miḷakāy eṉpatu mēlē vaṭārkkāṭṭu vaḻakku.
uṇa-uṇaṅku. uṇaṅkutal=ulartal.
tiṉaiviḷaittār muṟṟan tiṉaiyuṇaṅkum (tamiḻnā.154.)
uṇaṅkal = ularnta kūlam, vaṟṟal. uṇaṅku-uṇakkam.
uḷ-oḷ-oḷi-oḷir-oḷiṟu. oḷ-oṇmai = viḷakkam.  (va.va:91)
uṇmaiyiṉ iyalpu
uṇmai eṉpatu collai maṭṭum paṟṟiya aṟamaṉṟu. uḷḷum puṟamum ottiruppatē uṇmai. uḷḷam vāy mey ākiya mukkaraṇaṅkaḷum, oruṅkē uṇmaiyil ottiruttal vēṇṭum. ivviyalpaiyaṟintē, uṇmai, vāymai, meymmai eṉa uṇmaikku muppeyariṭṭaṉar muṉṉōr. uḷḷattaip paṟṟiyatu uṇmai. vāyaip paṟṟiyatu vāymai; meymaip paṟṟiyatu meymmai. (meymmai eṉṉuñ collai mey eṉṉum uṭampiṉ peyaraṭip piṟantatākak koḷḷutalāl poyyāṉa vuṭampai meyyeṉpatu maṅkala vaḻakku eṉa, ilakkaṇa uraiyāciriyar kūṟuvatu poruntātu.)
uṇmai collai maṭṭum paṟṟiyateṉṉum tappukkoḷkai yālēyē, pollātavar poyyāṇaiyiṭuvatum piṟar atai nampi oppukkoḷvatum, nērkiṉṟaṉa.
uḷattoṭu poruntātu colloṭum meyyoṭum maṭṭum poruntuvatu orupōtum uṇmaiyākātu. uḷattoṭu poruntiya uṇmaiyuraippavar tiruntiya voḻukka muṭaiya varāyiruttal tiṇṇam eṉṉum karuttē,
poyyāmai poyyāmai yāṟṟiṉ aṟampiṟa
coyyāmai ceyyāmai naṉṟu   (kuṟaḷ-297)
eṉṟa kuṟaḷiṉ aṭippaṭai eṉka.  (col : 110.)
uṇarvoli

uttaram, takkaṇam emmoḻic coṟkaḷ?
tamiḻaṉ piṟantakamākiya kumari nāṭṭil. mutaṟkaṇ tōṉṟiya nāṟṟicaip peyarkaḷ, teṉ, vaṭam, kīḻ mēl eṉpaṉa. avai 4ām vēṟṟumaikkuriya kuv vurupēṟṟut teṟku, vaṭakku, kiḻakku (kīḻkku), mēṟku eṉṟāki. piṉṉar āṭcipaṟṟi mutal vēṟṟumaiyākavē vaḻakkūṉṟi viṭṭaṉa.
avaṟṟuḷ, muṉṉiraṇṭum nilaittiṇaic ciṟappum, piṉ ṉiraṇṭum nila maṭṭamum paṟṟiyaṉa.
teṉ eṉpatu teṉṉai. teṉṉutal = kōṇutal. perum pālun teṉṉik koṇṭiruppatāl teṉṉai appeyar peṟṟatu. teṉ - teṉṉai, teṉ - teṉku - teṟku.
teṉṉai iyaṟkaiyākat tōṉṟiyatu kumarikkaṇṭattil. ikkālattil teṉṉaiyiṉ pala vakaikaḷ intō malaiyāviṉ iyaṟkaiyāka vaḷarvatāl an nilappakutiyē teṉṉaiyiṉ piṟantakamāyirukkalāmeṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. āyiṉ ap pakutiyum kumarik kaṇṭattaic cērntatēyeṉpatai aṟital vēṇṭum. paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟiṟkum kumariyāṟṟiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa eḻunūṟṟuk kāvata nilapparappiliruntu palvēṟu nāṭukaḷaik kuṟikkumiṭattu, aṭiyārkku nallār ēḻteṅka nāṭṭaiyē muṟpaṭak kuṟittiruttalāl. an nāṭukaḷ kumarikkaṇṭattiṉ teṉkōṭiyaṭuttiruntamai peṟappaṭum. teṉkōṭiyil teṉṉai ciṟappāka vaḷarntataṉālēyē, teṉṟicai appeyar peṟṟiruttal vēṇṭum.
iṅṅaṉamē nāvalan tēyattiṉ vaṭakōṭiyilum vaṭamaram (ālamaram) ciṟantu vaḷarntataṉāl. vaṭaticai appeyar peṟṟatu. vaṭanāvalap pakutiyāṉa vaṅkattil atu ciṟappāka vaḷarvatālēyē. mēlaip payirnūlārum ataṟku vaṅkāḷa atti (Ficushem-galensis) eṉa ilattīṉap peyariṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉar.
ālamaram cūḻa viḻutuviṭṭu vaṭṭamāka akaṉṟu vaḷarvatāl. vaṭam eṉap peyarpeṟṟatu. val-vaḷ-vaṭṭu-vaṭṭam-vaṭam.
o.nō : paṭṭam-paṭam = tuṇi māṉiyar villiyamcu camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akaramutaliyum, vaṭa (vata) eṉṉum ālamarap peyarukku vaṭṭam eṉpataiyē poruṭ karaṇiyamākak kūṟukiṉṟatu.
vaṭṭam - va vaṭa (vaṭṭa)
vaṭamoḻiyeṉṉum camaṟkirutattil, vaṭṭam eṉpatu vrutta eṉṟē yirukkumeṉpatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
vaṭṭam (tamiḻ) - vaṭṭa (pirākirutam) - vrut (camaṟkirutam)
nila maṭṭam iṟṟait tamiḻ nāṭṭiṟ pōṉṟē kumarik kaṇṭat tilum, mēṟkil uyarntum kiḻakkil tāḻntum iruntataṉāl, avviru ticaikaḷum muṟaiyē mēl kīḻ eṉappeyar peṟṟaṉa.
ulakil uyarnta paṉimalai. oru kālattil kaṭaṟkaraiyāy iruntatu. aṉṟu vaṭaticai kaṭalāl tāḻntum, teṉticai kumarimalaiyāl uyarntum, iruntaṉa. piṉṉar paṉimalai yeḻuntapiṉ iruticaiyum camamāyiṉa. ataṉpiṉ kumarimalai mūḻkiviṭavē, vaṭaticaiyuyarntum teṉṟicai tāḻntum pōyiṉa. ātalāl, vaṭaticaikku uttaram eṉṉum peyarum teṉṟicaikkut takkaṇam eṉṉum peyarum tōṉṟiṉa. avaṟṟoṭu ēṉaiyiru ticaikkum, kīḻ mēl eṉṉum paḻaiya karaṇiyam paṟṟiyē kuṇam kuṭam eṉṉum iru peyarkaḷum uṭaṉtōṉṟiṉa.
kuḷ-kuḻi. kuḷ-kuṇṭu = paḷḷam. kuḷ-kuṇ-kuṇam = paḷḷam. kuṇṭuṅ kuḻiyumāyirukkiṟatu eṉṉum vaḻakkai nōkkuka.
kuṭu-kuṭumi = ucci, māṭavucci, talaiyucci, uccikkoṇṭai, uccimuṭi, makuṭam, malaiyucci, koṭumuṭi. kuṭu-kuṭam = uyarcci, mēṭu, kuṭanāṭu = uyarnta malaināṭāṉa cēranāṭu, mēlnāṭu.
kiḻakku mēṟku eṉṉuñ coṟkaḷaiyottu. kuṇam kuṭam eṉpaṉavum kuṇakku kuṭakku eṉak kuvvurupēṟṟaṉa.
tamiḻ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya ulaka mutaṉmoḻiyātalāl, cēymai aṇmai muṉmai ākiya mūviṭattaiyuñ cuṭṭum ā (a) ī (i) ū (u) eṉṉum muccuṭṭum mutaṉmutal māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiya cuṭṭolikaḷāka maṭṭumiṉṟi uyiroli kaḷākavum irukkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉālēyē, avai tamiḻ neṭuṅ kaṇakkil mutalil vaikkappaṭṭiruppatuṭaṉ, iṉṟum āriyamoḻikaḷiṟpōl maṟaiyātum tiriyātum iyalpākavē tamiḻil vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. cuṭṭuvakaiyilum ataṉ vaḻippaṭṭa mūviṭap peyar vakaiyilum, tamiḻ (tiraviṭam) āriyattiṟku (camaṟkiruttiṟku) muntiya teṉṟum, ulaka mutaṉ moḻiyōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpuṭaiya teṉṟum, kālṭuvelār tam oppiyalilakkaṇattil āṅkāṅku mikaveṭutturaittuc cellutal kāṇka.
muṉṉilaip peyar: ūṉ-uṉ (orumai) ūm-um (paṉmai)
uvaṉ, uvaḷ, uvar, utu, uvai, uṅku eṉpaṉa muṉṉilaip poruḷkaḷaiyum iṭattaiyumē kuṟittalāl, ukara allatu ūkāraccuṭṭu mutaṉmaic cuṭṭē. muṉmai cēymaikkum aṇ maikkum iṭaippaṭṭa tātalāl, iṭaimaic cuṭṭeṉpatu orumaruṅku poruntum. āyiṉ, cēymaikku varampillai yātalāl, muṉmai iṭaimaiyākalām. iṭaimai eṉṟum muṉmaiyākātu.
muṉmai aṇmaikkuc caṟṟuc cēymaiyātalāl muṉmaic cuṭṭu, tamiḻiṟ ciṟupāṉmai ciṟu cēymaiyaiyum vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷiṟ cēymaiyaiyum uṇarttum.

Uttara, mfn cp.fr.1.ud; opposed to adbara. upper, higher, superior (e.g. uttare dantas, the upper teeth), RV, AV, TS. CHUP; Ragh, and c, northern (because the northern part of India is high), AV; Mn; Susr.; pancat & c. eṉṟu māṉiyar villiyamcu camaṟkiruta - āṅkila akaramutali kūṟutal kāṇka.

vaṭamoḻiyāḷar vaṭavai eṉṉum teṉcollai, vaṭavā, vaṭavā mukam, vaṭavāmukākkiṉi, paṭapā, paṭapāmukam, paṭapā mukākkaṉi eṉṟu tirittum virittum, peṭṭaikkutirai mukattil tōṉṟiya neruppeṉṟu, poruntāp poytta lākap poruḷkūṟuvar. atait tamiḻar nampiya piṉṉarē. vaṭavaik kaṉaṉmāvum (takkayākap.694) eṉṟu oṭṭak kūttarum pāṭanērntatu. itaṉāl, tamiḻccol tirikkappaṭṭa toṭu tamiḻar matiyum tirikkappaṭṭamai kāṇka.

takku - takkaṇam = nilamaṭṭattil tāḻnta teṉṟicai. teṉṉāṭu (deccan) 
takkaṇam - va.takṣiṇam
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar, takṣiṇam eṉṉum collait teṟku eṉṉum poruḷil, takṣiṇākkiṉi, takṣiṇāyaṉam, takṣiṇā calam (potiyam), takṣiṇa pāñcālam, takṣiṇāmūrtti eṉa āḷuvar āyiṉ, takṣiṇam eṉṉum colliṟkut takṣ eṉpatai mūlamākakkoṇṭu ataṟkut tiṟamaiyāyiruttal allatu valamaiyōṭiruttal eṉṟu poruḷkūṟi, kiḻakku nōkkum pōtu teṉṟicai valappuṟamum vaṭaticai iṭappuṟamum iruppatāl takṣiṇam eṉṉum coṟku valam eṉṟum uttaram eṉṉum coṟku iṭam eṉṟum poruḷ tōṉṟiṉaveṉṟu karaṇiyaṅ kāṭṭuvar.
takṣiṇam pari = valam vā. valañcuṟṟu.
pratakṣiṇa = valam varukai, valañ cuṟṟukai.
takutiyeṉṉuñ coṟku vallamaiyeṉṟum poruḷiruppatāl takṣ eṉṉum vaṭacol taku eṉṉun teṉcolliṉ tiripākavumirukkalām.
o.nō. : L.dexter = of or on the right-hand side.
E. dexterous = deft, adroit, clever, using right hand by preference
E, ME. deft = dexterous, skilful.
E, daft. ME.daffte = OE ged & fte (Goth gadaban to be fit) orig. Sense, fitting, suitable.
takṣ eṉṉum vaṭacoṟkum. (dexter) eṉṉum ilattīṉac coṟkum. mūlam etuvāyiṉum, uttaram, takkaṇam eṉṉum iru ticaiccollum teṉcollēyeṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟu kaḷāvaṉa:-
1. uttaram, takkaṇam eṉpaṉavaṟṟōṭu cērntu vaḻaṅkappeṟum kuṇam kuṭam eṉṉum ticaippeyarkaḷ tūya teṉcol. avai vaṭamoḻiyil illai. avaṟṟōṭu cērnta ēṉai yiraṇṭum teṉcollākavēyiruttal vēṇṭum.
2. uyarntōṉ tāḻntōṉ eṉpaṉa pōla, uttaram takkaṇam eṉṉum iraṇṭum oṉṟiṟkoṉṟu etirāṉa uṟaviyaṟ kuṟiyīṭukaḷ (relative terms.)
camaṟkirutattil. uttaram eṉpataṟku nilavuyarcciyaiyum takṣiṇam eṉpataṟku valappuṟattaiyum, karaṇiyamākak kāṭṭuvatu poruntātu.
3. uttaram eṉṉum colliṟku aṭiyāṉa ukarac cuṭṭum. takkaṇam eṉṉum colliṟku mūlamāṉa takku eṉṉum mutaṉilaiyum tūya tamiḻ.
4. kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya tamiḻ camaṟkirutattiṟku muntiyatu.
5. uttiram takkaṇam eṉṉum nāṭṭuppeyarkaḷ allatu ticaippeyarkaḷ, tamiḻarum avar vaḻiyiṉarāṉa tiraviṭarum toṉṟutoṭṭu vāḻum nāvalan tēyattilaṉṟi, vēṟev vāriya nāṭṭilum eṉṟēṉum vaḻaṅkiyatillai.
kaṭavuḷ vaḻipāṭiṉṟik katiravaṉ vaṇakkattaiyē ciṟappākak koṇṭa vētaāriyar. intiyāviṟkuṭ pukuntapiṉṉarē, kālaik katirava vaṇakkattiṉ pōtu takkaṇam valappuṟamum uttaram iṭappuṟamum iruttal kaṇṭu. avviruticaip peyar kaṭkum muṟaiyē valam iṭam eṉap poruḷ koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭum.
kiḻakku mutaliya muṉṟōṉṟiya nāṟṟicaip peyarum ulaka vaḻakkil āḻa ūṉṟiviṭṭataṉāl, kuṇakku (kuṇam) mutaliya piṟṟōṉṟiya nāṟṟicaip peyarum ceyyuḷ vaḻakkeṉṉum ilakkiya vaḻakkilēyē iṭampeṟalāyiṉa.
ulaka vaḻakkiliṉmaiyālum poyyuṭai vaṭavar col vaṉmaiyiṉālumē, uttaram takkaṇam eṉṉum iru teṉ coṟkaḷum vaṭacolleṉum mayakkattiṟku iṭamāyiṉaveṉa, ikkaṭṭuraiyiṉiṉṟum teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
mutaltaram, kaṭaittaram, mēltaram, kīḻttaram, naṭuttaram, iṭaittaram, ciṟutaram, peruntaram, eṉpaṉa pōṉṟatē uttaram (uyarnta nilai) eṉṉum vaḻakkum eṉka. vaṭamoḻiyāḷar ivvaṭivai uṟaḻtaram (comparative degree) ākkik koṇṭaṉar. ataṉāl ut+tara (ud+tara) eṉṟu pakuppar. atukaṇṭu mayaṅkaṟka.
muraṭṭuk kuṟukkup perumaram uttaram eṉṟu peyarpeṟ ṟiruttalaiyum nōkkuka.  - maturai eḻuttāḷar maṉṟam 16ām āṇṭuviḻāc ciṟappumalar 1974.

umpar
neṭuñcēṇ veḷināṭṭārāṉa kālṭu velār tolkāppiya mum kaṭaik kaḻakap paṉuvalkaḷum tamiḻp perum pulavarkkut teriyātu maṟaintum, tamiḻaṉ piṟantakam kumari nāṭeṉṉuṅ karuttu oruvaruḷḷattilum tōṉṟa vaḻiyillātum, irunta kālattil, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ pōlum vaḻikāṭṭiyār oruvaru miṉṟit tāmē tamiyarāyt tamiḻaik kaṟṟāyntārēṉum, naṭu nilaiyāka āyntataṉāl, tamiḻiṉ ulaka mutaṉmaiyaik kaṇṭu, tiraviṭac cuṭṭukaḷ camaṟkirutattiṉiṉṟu kaṭaṉkoḷḷappaṭṭa vaiyalla. ataṟku māṟāka, avai, camaṟkirutac cuṭṭukaṭkum ata ṉoṭu cērnta vēṟupala intiya-airōppiyak kuṭumpa moḻic cuṭṭukaṭkum mūlamāṉa, yāppēttiyac (Japhetic) cuṭṭaṭikaḷai ottuḷḷaṉa. (tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ oppilakkaṇam, ceṉṉaip patippu, pak.422) eṉṟu tiṇṇamuṟak kūṟiyuḷḷār.
camaṟkirutattiṉ taṉiyuṭaimaiyallāta nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa aṭippaṭaic coṟkaḷ, ataṟkum tamiḻukkum potuvāka vuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ perumpālāṉa tamiḻ eṉpatu, aṟiviyaṟ colliyalāl iṉṟu veṭṭa veḷiyākiṉṟatu. vaṭamoḻi yāḷar kuṟikkōṭ colliyal (Tendentious Etymology) koṇṭē, avaṟṟaiyellām vaṭacol leṉṟē ituvarai ēmāṟṟi vantirukkiṉṟaṉar.
āriya moḻic cuṭṭukaṭkellām tamiḻc cuṭṭukaḷē aṭip paṭaiyātalāl, vēṟu poruṭ coṟkaḷai vaṭamoḻiyāḷar uṭaṉaṭiyāy oppukkoḷḷāviṭiṉum, cuṭṭupporuṭ coṟkaḷait tamiḻc colleṉṟu oppuk koḷḷāmalirukka muṭiyātu.
tamiḻc cuṭṭukaḷ, mūvēṟiṭattait tappātu cuṭṭum mūvēṟu taṉiyeḻuttolikaḷ allatu ōreḻuttuc coṟkaḷ; āriyac cuṭṭukaḷō perumpālum iṭamāṟik kuṟippaṉavum, īriṭat tiṟkup potuvāṉavum, aṭi tirintaṉavumāṉa palaveḻuttuc coṟkaḷ. iḵtoṉṟē tamiḻccuṭṭiṉ muṉmai kāṭṭac cālum.
āriyamoḻikaḷiṟ ceṉṟu vaḻaṅkum tamiḻc cuṭṭuc coṟkaḷ palaviruppiṉum, iṅku umpar eṉṉuñ collē eṭuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭatu.
eṇpeyar muṟaipiṟap puruvamāttirai mutalī ṟiṭainilai pōli yeṉṉum pattu akattilakkaṇattiṟkum, kiḷavi puṇarcci yeṉṉum iru puṟattilakkaṇattiṟkum, iṭamāṉatum; uyirmey uyirmey eṉṉum pākupāṭum uyir meyt taṉivaṭivum uṭaiyatum, mutaṉmutal māntaṉ vāyil tōṉṟiya ā ī ū (a i u) eṉṉum muccuṭṭukaḷaiyē mutaṟkaṇ koṇṭatumāṉa neṭuṅkaṇakku, ulakil mutaṉ mutal tōṉṟiyatu tamiḻilēyē tamiḻneṭuṅ kaṇakkiṉ vaḷarcciyē vaṭamoḻi vaṇṇamālai. itaip piṟaḻa vuṇarntār kālṭuvelār.
ā ī ū eṉṉum mūṉṟum, muṟaiyē, cēymaic cuṭṭum aṇmaiccuṭṭum ivviraṇṭiṟkum iṭaippaṭṭa muṉmaic cuṭṭum ākum. ivaṟṟuḷ ētēṉum oṉṟai olikkum nilaiyil, ēṉaiyiraṇṭaiyum olikka viyalātu. ātalāl, ivai viralpōṟ cuṭṭikaḷ (Pointers) āyiṉa. itu tamiḻiṉ allatu tirāviṭattiṉ ciṟappiyalpu.
māntaṉ iyakkam allatu celavu nilattiṉmēlum malaiyiṉmēlum nikaḻvatāl, muṉmaiccuṭṭu iṭam nōkki uyirccic cuṭṭumākum.

vētamoḻiyai vēta camaṟkirutam (Vedic Sanskrit) eṉṟu colvatu, muṟkālap paṭuttam (Prochronism) eṉṉuṅ kuṟṟa mākum. vētamoḻiyum camaṟkirutamum oru kālattil vaḻakku moḻiyāyiruntaṉa eṉṟu mēlē moḻinūlār koḷvatu, tamiḻiṉ kumarināṭṭut tōṟṟattaiyum muṉmaiyaiyum aṟiyāmaiyāl nērnta viḷaivē.
intiyiluḷḷa ūppar eṉṉuñ col, ammoḻikku nēr mūlamāṉa cūracēṉip pirākirutattilum, perumpālum uppar eṉṟu vaḻaṅkiyiruttal vēṇṭum. atiliruntu ilattīṉattiṟkuc ceṉṟu cupper (Super) eṉṟu tirintatu. coṟkaḷiṉ uyirmutal iṅṅaṉam cakara meyyēṟuvatu iyalpē.

makkaḷiṉ karuttukkaḷai olivaṭivāṉa coṟkaḷ terivik kiṉṟaṉa; māntaṉ aṟivu vaḷara vaḷara, karuttukkaḷ oṉṟilirun teṉṟākak kiḷaikkiṉṟaṉa. mūlak karuttaik kuṟikkuñ colli ṉiṉṟu kiḷaik karuttaik kuṟikkuñ collai vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭa, paṟpala vakaiyiṟ palvēṟu muṟaikaḷ kaiyāḷappaṭṭuḷa. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu eḻuttut tiripu. colliṉ mutaliṭai kaṭai yeṉṉum mūviṭattilumuḷḷa uyir mey eṉṉum iruvakai yeḻuttum, palvēṟeḻuttākat tiriyum. uyireḻuttukkaḷuḷ oṉṟāṉa ukaram ēṉai nāṟkuṟilākavut tiriyum.
