pakaram jakaramāvatu inti āriya moḻi yiyalpu.
e-ṭu: inti. ít‹ (t.)-jath‹, yātrā-jātrā-yōki-jōki.
t.bastd-L. juvenilis, E.juvenile.
va. íf (g.)-E. yoke, L.jugo, conjugo, to yoke to gether. E. conjugate.
iy-va. i (i), to go, walk; to flow; to blow; to advance, spread, get about; to go to or towards (with acc.) come, RV.; AV.; S Br.; M Bh.; R.; Hit; Ragh. & c.; to go away, escape, pass, retire, RV.; AV.; SBr, to arise from, come from, RV.; Ch up.; to return (in this sense only fut). M Bh.; R.; (with punar) to come back again, return, M Bh.; R.; Pancat. & c.; to succeed, Mn. iii, 127; to arrive at, reach, obtain, RV.; AV.; SBr.; Sak.; Hit. & c.; to fall into, come to.; to approach with prayers, gain by asking (of. ita); to undertake anything (with acc.); to be employed in, go on with, continue in any condition or relation (with a part, or instr., e.g. asura rakshasani mridyananani yanti, “the Asuras and Rakshasas are being continually crushed,” SBr. i, 1, 4, 14.
(māṉiyar ulliyamcu camaṟkiruta-āṅkila, akara mutali pak.163).
ē-va. V (a) to go, come near or towards, go near, approach, RV.; AV.; SBr. etc.; to reach, attain, enter, come into (a state or position), Mn. xil, 125.
eta, come near, approached, RV.
iti, arrival, approach, RV. x, 91:4  ( 227).
uyartiṇai
pakuttaṟivuḷḷa uyirkaḷaik kuṟikkum coṟkaḷai uyartiṇai eṉṟu vakuttaṉar.  (col.34.)
uyir
uṭampai uyppatu allatu celuttuvatu uyir. uḷḷirukkum uyirai mēlākap potintirukkum uṭampu mey. uyirai yuṭaiya mey, uyirmey.
(uyirum meyyum kūṭiyatu uyirmey eṉpatu poruntātu. ikkūṟṟu uyirmey eṉṉum col ulakavaḻakkaṟṟapiṉ eḻuntatu. pirāṇaṉaiyuṭaiyatu pirāṇi eṉpatu pōl uyirai yuṭaiya mey uyirmey eṉpatē poruttamām.)  (col.31)
uyirmey
eḻuttukkaḷil, vēṟōr eḻuttiṉ utaviyiṉṟit tāṉē olikkum uyireḻuttu tāṉāy iyaṅkum uyiraiyum, uyireḻut tiṉ utaviyiṉṟit taṉit toliyāta meyyeḻuttu uyiriṉṟi iyaṅkāta uṭampaiyum, uyirōṭu kūṭiya meyyeḻuttu uyirōṭu kūṭiya uṭampaiyum ottiruttalāl ammūvakai eḻuttiṟkum ammūvakaip poruṭ peyarkaḷaiyē uvamai yāku peyarāka iṭṭaṉar mutaṉūlāciriyar.  (col. 34.)
uyirmey varivaṭivukaḷiṉ ōriyaliṉmai
toṭakka vakuppil eḻuttu eḻutip payilum ciṟupiḷḷaikku. ka, kā, ṅa, ṅā, ca, cā, ña, ñā, ṭa, ṭā eṉṟu eḻutikkāṭṭi, ṇav viṟku aṭutta eḻuttai eḻutac coṉṉāl, ṇā eṉṟutāṉ eḻutum. itaṉāl, ōriyalāka eḻutuvatē iyaṟkai yeṉṟum, muntiya vakaiyeṉṟum, ciṟupiḷḷaikkum collāmalē teriyum eṉṟum, aṟintukoḷḷalām.
ivviyaṟkai muṟaiyai māṟṟi ēṉ eḻutiṉar muṉṉōr allatu ilakkaṇa āciriyar eṉiṉ. kūṭṭeḻuttil allatu viraiveḻuttil ṇavvaṭivuṅ kāluñ cērtteḻutiṉ. kālum oru kōṇac cuḻipōl tōṉṟi ṇakara ākārattai iru ṉakaramākak karuti mayaṅka iṭantaru mātaliṉ. atai nīkkutaṟkē eṉka.
i, ī, u, ū, e, ē, au eṉṉum ēḻuyirum ēṟum ṇakara mey vaṭivukaḷ. attakaiya mayakkattiṟku iṭantarāmaiyāl, ti, tī, tu, tū, te, tē, tau eṉpaṉa pōṉṟē ṇi, ṇī, ṇu, ṇū, ṇe, ṇē, ṇau eṉṟu iyaṟkaiyāṉa muṟaiyil eḻutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
aikāra vuyirmeykku ṉakarampōl tōṉṟum iraṭṭaik kompum, okara ōkārameykaṭkuk kompuṅ kālum. iyaṟkai muṟaiyil variṉ, pala cuḻikaḷ pōl tōṉṟi mēṟkūṟiya mayakkattai mikukkumātaliṉ, avvuyir meykaḷ vēṟupaṭṭa vaṭivil eḻutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iru allatu mukkōṇac cuḻiyeḻuttukaḷ pōṉṟē iru vaḷaivuḷḷa eḻuttukaḷum cilaviṭattu mayakkat taruvaṉavā yiruttalāl, attakaiya iṭaṅkaḷil avaiyum iyaṟkaikku māṟāṉa vaṭivil eḻutappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ṟakara vaṭivu iruvaḷaivuḷḷatā yiruntum. ēṉ aikāra vuyirmey vaṭivil, ṟai eṉṟē iyaṟkai vaṭivil eḻutappaṭukiṉṟa teṉiṉ. ataṟkuk kōṭu kīḻiṟaṅkiyiruppatāl mayakkattiṟkiṭamillātu taṭukkiṉṟa teṉka. itaṉāl, iṉṟiyamaiyāta iṭaṅkaḷilēyē aikāra vuyirmeykkuk kokkuk kaḻuttup pōṉṟa mēṟ koṭukkuk koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar eṉpatai aṟika.
iṉi, āṅkilattil illāta uyirmeyyeḻuttukaḷ tamiḻil iruppatāl, eḻuttukaḷ palkit taṭṭaccaṭippil mikunta iṭarppāṭuṇṭākkukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, tolaivari yaṭippuk kālattai nīṭṭik kiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum, kuṟaikūṟit tamiḻeḻuttu kaḷaik kuṟaikka vēṇṭum eṉpar cilar.
vaṭamoḻi neṭuṅkaṇakkilum ataip piṉpaṟṟiya vaṭanāṭṭu moḻikaḷilum tirāviṭa moḻikaḷilum uḷḷa vaṇṇamālai kaḷilum, valliṉam aintum nannāṉku vakaiyāyiruppatoṭu. cakarattiṟkiṉamāṉa icuppolikaḷum (sibilants) mūṉṟāka vuḷḷaṉa.
uyirmeykaḷiṉ ukarakkuṟi, eḻuttu vaṭiviṟkēṟpat teluṅ kiluṅ kaṉṉaṭattilum iruvēṟu vakaiyāyum, malaiyāḷattil nālvēṟu vakaiyākavum uḷḷatu.
cēmiya moḻikaḷil uyirmeykkut taṉivaṭivillāviṭiṉum. meyvaṭivu coṉ mutaliṭaikaṭai yeṉṉum mūviṭattiṟ kēṟpap perumpālum vēṟupaṭukiṉṟatu. mēlum, paṟpala eḻuttukaḷiṉ vaṭivukaḷ ovvoṉṟākavēyiruntu. puḷḷikaḷiṉ mēlkīḻuḷ eṉṉum iṭavēṟupāṭṭālum, oṉṟu iraṇṭu mūṉṟu nāṉku eṉṉum tokai vēṟupāṭṭālumē. vēṟu pakuttaṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṉi, a i u eṉṉum mutaṉ mūvuyirk kum taṉi vaṭivukaḷillaiyeṉṟu karutappaṭukiṉṟatu. oru kuṟuṅkīccu mēlum kīḻum taṭittum eḻutappaṭuvatāl, muṟaiyē mūvuyirk kuṟilkaḷum kuṟikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ neṭil kaḷaik kuṟikka alip (alep), vā, yē eṉṉum eḻuttukaḷ muṟaiyē cērkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vā eṉṉum orēyeḻuttu vakaramākavum ukaramākavum, yē eṉṉum orēyeḻuttu yakaramākavum ikaramākavum payaṉpaṭuttap paṭukiṉṟaṉa.
āṅkila vaṇṇamālaiyiṉ oḻuṅkiṉmai aṉaivarum aṟintatē.
In the English tongue we speak
Why is break not rhymed with freak
Well you tell me why it's true
We say sew but likewise jew.
Beard sounds not the same as heard
Cord is different from word
And since pay is rhymed with say
Why not paid with said I pray.
Cow is cow but low is low
Shoe is never rhymed with foe
And in short it seems to me
Sound and letter disagree.
eṉṟu Lord Cromer eṉṉum ōr āṅkilap perumakaṉārē pāṭi yuḷḷār. āyiṉum, oruvarum atait tiruttak karutavillai.
īriṭamē koḷkiḷum eḻuttukaḷai ṇo, ṇō, ṟo, ṟō eṉṟeḻutiṉ mūviṭaṅ koḷḷum, kāl cērkkāmaiyāl avai muṭampaṭṭu naṭakka muṭiyātu varuntavumillai; kālvēṇṭumeṉṟu nammiṭam muṟaiyiṭavumillai.
eḻuttu māṟṟam evvakaiyilun tamiḻukkut tēvaiyēyillai.
iṉṟu eḻuttai māṟṟiṉ, maṉaiyakaṅkaḷilum aluvalakaṅ kaḷilum nūlakaṅkaḷilumuḷḷa kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa marapeḻuttu nūlkaḷaiyellām māṟṟiyākal vēṇṭum. avaṟṟiṟku īṭu ceyya iṟaivaṉum varāṉ. putiya māṟṟeḻuttu nūlkaḷai yellām acciṭa oru talaimuṟaiyākum; kōṭik kaṇakkāṉa paṇamuñ celavaḻiyum. avaṟṟaik koṭukkum vaḷḷalkaḷ iṉimēltāṉ piṟakka vēṇṭum.
ēṟkeṉavēyuḷḷa nūlkaḷai vēṇṭumāyiṉ, karivalam vanta nallūriṟ pōl eriyūṭṭuvataṟkut tōṉṟiyaṅkaḷ (ākamaṅkaḷ) tuṇai ceyyalām. āyiṉ, ataṟkum āriyamaṟai yōtu vārkkup paṇaṅkoṭuttal vēṇṭum.
eḻuttu māṟṟuvatāl oru naṉmaiyumillai. kēṭō aḷavaṟṟa tākum.
iṉi, eḻuttu māṟṟamum oruvakaippaṭṭataṉṟu. periyār eḻuttumuṟai oṉṟu; piṟarkūṟum eḻuttumuṟai vevvēṟu. uyirmey varivaṭivukaḷai. oruvar kirantam pōṟ puṇarkka vēṇṭum eṉpar. oruvar vaṭamoḻipōṟ puṇarkka vēṇṭum eṉpar oruvar pirāmipōṟ puṇarkka vēṇṭum eṉpar. piṟar vevvēṟu vakaiyiṟ puṇarkka vēṇṭum eṉpar. orucilar uyirmeyyē vēṇṭiyatillaiyeṉṟum, āṅkilampōl meyyaiyum uyiraiyum aṭuttaṭut teḻutiṉāṟpōtum eṉṟum kūṟuvar.
iṅṅaṉam vaṭivupaṟṟiya karuttu vēṟupāṭukaḷ orupakkam irukka, cilar piṟamoḻic ciṟappeḻuttukaḷait tamiḻiṟ pukutta vēṇṭumeṉpar. avarum, vaṭamoḻic ciṟappeḻuttukaḷai maṭṭum pukutta vēṇṭum eṉpārum, āṅkilac ciṟappeḻuttu kaḷai maṭṭum pukutta vēṇṭum eṉpārum, piṟamoḻic ciṟappeḻuttukaḷaiyuñ cērkka vēṇṭum eṉpārumāyp pala tiṟattar.
 pāṇṭiyar mutaliru kaḻakam pōṉṟa toṉṟiṉmaiyāl, iṅṅaṉam pēccurimaiyum eḻutturimaiyum varampiṉṟip pāḻākkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉṟu eḻuttu māṟṟattai virumpuvār takuti nōkkiṉ, perum pulamaiyum marapukākkum poṟupputtaṉmaiyum oruvarkku millai yeṉṟē teriyavarum.
tamiḻavēḷ umāmakēcuvaram piḷḷai avarkaḷ,
10, 11-6-1934 aṉṟu, tirunelvēli intuk kallūriyil naṭaipeṟṟa ceṉṉai mānilat tamiḻar mutal mānāṭṭil talaimai tāṅki, āṟṟiya coṟpoḻivil oru pakuti varumāṟu:
paḻantamiḻk kālamutal vaṭacoṟkaḷum ticaiccoṟkaḷum tamiḻil iṉṟiyamaiyātaviṭattu vaḻaṅki vantirukkiṉṟaṉa. āṉāl, avvāṟu vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟapōtu, avai tamiḻiyalukku ēṟpa urumāṟit toḻil puriya vēṇṭumeṉpatu āṉṟōr vakutta tamiḻmarapu. vaṭacoṟ kiḷavi vaṭaveḻut torīi eḻuttōṭu puṇarnta collā kummē eṉpatu nūṟpā. vaṭaveḻuttukaḷiṟ pala tamiḻiyalukku muraṇpaṭutaliṉ, avaṟṟai nīkki eḻutuvatē muṟaiyeṉa mutaṟiñar kaṇṭaṉar. vaṭacoṟkaḷ peritum vaḻaṅkat talaippaṭṭa piṟkālattilum, iv viyalukku māṟupaṭā vaṇṇam naṉṉūlār viti vakuttār. caṅkāla mutal itukāṟum tōṉṟivarum ciṟanta nūlkaḷ yāvum, im moḻimarapu vaḻuvāmal pātukāttu varukiṉṟaṉa. oru cilar, tamiḻ eḻuttilakkaṇattil kuṟaipāṭukaḷ malintuḷḷaṉaveṉ ṟum, avaṟṟai akaṟṟa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. kurōmar eṉṟa āṅkilap pulavar tam moḻiyiṉ eḻuttilak kaṇattiṟ kāṇappeṟum cila kuṟaikaḷait tokuttu oru kaviyil viḷakkiyuḷḷār. eṉiṉum, āṅkila moḻiyiṉ vaḷarcci ciṟituṅkuṉṟavillai. accukkōppōriṉ tuṉpattiṟkākac cilar, tamiḻ eḻuttukaḷiṟ cilavaṟṟai akaṟṟa virumpukiṉṟaṉar. vēṟu cilar ‘f’ eṉṟa āṅkila eḻuttaiyum tamiḻiṟ cērkkak karutukiṉṟaṉar. ivai emmoḻiyāḷarum kaikkoḷḷātavai. vīramāmuṉivar pōppaiyar pōṉṟa ayalnāṭṭārkūṭa ivvāṟu collat tuṇiyavillai. vaṭakalai teṉkalai uṇarnta nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, parimēlaḻakar pōṉṟār vaṭa nūlār koḷkaiyil aṭippaṭṭiruntum, tamiḻamarapu iḻukkā vaṇṇam urainūlkaḷ iyaṟṟiṉārkaḷē! āṅkilam, vaṭa moḻi ākiyavaṟṟil cīrtiruttam ceyya nirampa iṭamiruntum avaṟṟait tiruttat tuṇiyātapōtu, tamiḻmoḻiyait tiruttalāmeṉpatu pētaimaiyākum. kalainūlkaḷil vaḻaṅkum kuṟiyīṭṭuc coṟkaḷukku ēṟṟa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ nūlilō vaḻakkilō kiṭaittāl, avaṟṟaip payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭum; illātapōtu tamiḻil paṭaittuk koḷḷa vēṇṭum.... cerumāṉiyar aṟivunūṟ poruḷkaḷaik kuṟippataṟkāṉa coṟkaḷait tam tāymoḻiyilēyē ākkik koṇṭaṉar. im muṟaiyait tamiḻarum piṉpaṟṟalām.
perum pulavarum perun tamiḻkkāvalarum iṅṅaṉam eḻuttu māṟṟattai etirppatu kavaṉikkattakkatu.
periyār oru periyār, avar toṇṭu eḻuttumāṟṟamaṉṟu. ceyaṟkariya ceyvatē periyār iyalpu. pirāmaṇiyattaip pōkkuvatum pakuttaṟivaip pukaṭṭuvatum mūṭap paḻakkavaḻakkaṅ kaḷai oḻippatum tamiḻarait taṉmāṉattoṭu vāḻaccey vatumē periyāriṉ uṇmait toṇṭu. viṭutalai, kuṭiyaracu mutaliya kiḻamaiyaṉkaḷiṉ eḻuttu māṟṟam cikkaṉam paṟṟiyatē. iṉṟu periyāriṉ paṭaittalaivarpōl tammaik kāṭṭik koḷpavar. iḷaiyarum muḷaiyarumāyirunta kālattē. nāṉ periyārōṭu neruṅkiya toṭarpukoṇṭavaṉ. eṉṟaikku inti kaṭṭāyap pāṭamākap pukuttappaṭṭatō, aṉṟaikkē eṉakkup periyār toṭarpu toṭaṅkiṟṟu. nāṉ tamiḻnalam paṟṟi ētēṉuñ coṉṉāl, atellām nīṅkaḷē tamiḻp paṇṭitarkaḷākac cērntukoṇṭu kiḷarcci ceyyuṅkaḷ. nāṉ uṅkaḷaippōlap paṇṭitaṉallēṉ. paṭiyāta (pāmara) makka ḷiṭam ceṉṟu avarkaḷuṭaiya aṟiyāmaiyai eṭuttukkāṭṭi eṉṉāliyaṉṟa varai camutāyattoṇṭu ceypavaṉ eṉpār. orumuṟai eṉ oppiyaṉ moḻinūlpaṟṟi īrōṭṭiliruntu 5 pakkam tam kaippaṭa eḻutiyiruntār. ap pottakamum 100 paṭikaḷ eṉṉiṭam vilaikku vāṅkiṉār. cilamuṟai avar kūṭṭattiṟkut talaimai tāṅkiyumirukkiṟēṉ.
ōrumuṟai, nāṉ kāṭṭuppāṭiyiliruntapōtu, eṉakku varuvāyillaiyeṉṟu terintu. eṉ vīṭu tēṭik koñcam paṇaṅkoṭukka vantu, nāṉ ūril illātāl akkam pakkattiluḷḷavariṭam ceytiyaic colliviṭṭuc ceṉṟu viṭṭār.
piṉpu, nāṉ tiruccirāppaḷḷi ceṉṟiruntapōtu naṇparuṭaṉ cērntu periyāraik kāṇac ceṉṟēṉ. avar eṉṉait taṉiyāyt tam māḷikaikku uḷḷē aḻaittu, irunūṟu urupā naṉkoṭaiyākat tantār. atu oru ciṟu tokaiyē yāyiṉum piṟariṭattiṟ peṟum īrāyirattiṟkuc camam eṉpatu. avar cikkaṉattai yaṟinta aṉaivarum uṇarvar.
nāṉ periyārai matippatellām, evarukkum añcāmaiyum etaiyum potumakkaṭku eṭuttu viḷakkum āṟṟalum paṟṟiyē. kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷoṭu kūṭikkoṇṭu kumpalil kōvintā pōṭuvatupōl, pērāyattār āṅkilarāṭciyai etirttatu attuṇai āṇmaiyaṉṟu. kumpakōṇamāyiṉum kumarikkōṭṭak kāciyāyiṉum, caṟṟum añcātu pirāma ṇiyattaic cāṭuvatilum ataṉ koṭumaikaḷaik kallā māntarkku viḷakkik kūṟuvatilum. ōyvu cāyviṉṟi iṉanalattaip pēṇuvatilum, avarukku īṭāṉavar ituvarai iruntatu millai; iṉiyirukkap pōvatumillai. pirittāṉiyattai yetirttatilum pirāmaṇiyattai yetirttatē pērāṇmai.
kalappu maṇam, pakuttaṟivuc ceyal, taṉmāṉa vāḻvu mutaliya uyirnāṭik koḷkaikaḷai viṭṭuviṭṭu, eḻuttu māṟṟam oṉṟaiyē mēṟkoḷvatu. paṇṭattai viṭṭuviṭṭu paṭivattaip paṟṟuvatē yākum. taṉittamiḻai veṟuppavarum uṇmaiyāṉa varalāṟṟai oppuk koḷḷātavarum intiyaip potumoḻi yāka ēṟpavarumāṉa vaiyā purikaḷuṭaṉ kūṭikkoḷvatum, tamiḻukku māṟāṉa āriya amaippakaṅkaḷuṭaṉ ottuḻaippa tum, mūlamum paṭiyum eṉpatai acalum nakalum eṉṟeḻutu vatum, pakuttaṟivuk koḷkaiyiṉ allatu taṉmāṉa vāḻviṉ pāṟpaṭṭaṉa vākā.
periyāriṉ naṭattaiyaip piṉpaṟṟātu. periyār viḻāk koṇṭāṭṭattil ūrtoṟum ūrvalattiṟ kalantu koḷ vatum, viṭiya viṭiyac coṟpoḻivāṟṟuvatum, periyār paṭimaikku mālaiyaṇivatum, peyar viḷamparattiṟkē yaṉṟi vēṟetaṟkup payaṉām?
itukāṟum tamiḻnāṭṭil, tamiḻarkku mūvēṟu vakaiyil vaḻikāṭṭa mūvēṟu periyār tōṉṟiyuḷḷaṉar. avaruḷ oruvar ī.vē.irāmacāmip periyār. eḻuttu māṟṟattaiyē avar toṇṭākak kāṭṭupavar. avar perumaikku iḻukkē tēṭupavarāvar; tam ciṟu koḷkaikkup peṟu veṟṟi peṟavē, periyār viḻāvaip peruvāyppākak koṇṭuḷḷaṉar.
uram
ur-uṟu = mikka. uṟu-uṟuti = valimai.
ur-uram = valimai, nilattiṟku allatu payirukku valimai tarum eru, uṭampil valiya uṟuppāṉa mārpu.
iṉi, uram = taḻuvum uṟuppu eṉṟumām. ur=uṟu.
uṟutal = poruntutal, taḻuvutal.
o.nō : maru (maruvu)-mār-mārpu-mārpam. maruvutal = poruntutal, taḻuvutal.
vaṭavar kāṭṭum ru (r) eṉṉum mūlam poruntāmai kāṇka.  (va.va: 93)
uriccol viḷakkam
uriccoṟ kiḷavi virikkuṅ kālai
icaiyiṉum kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum tōṉṟip
peyariṉum viṉaiyiṉum meytaṭu māṟi
orucoṟ palaporuṭ kurimai tōṉṟiṉum
palacol oruporuṭ kurimai tōṉṟiṉum
payilā tavaṟṟaip payiṉṟavai cārttit
tattam marapiṟ ceṉṟunilai maruṅkiṉ
eccol lāyiṉum poruḷvēṟu kiḷattal   (tol.782)
eṉpatu tolkāppiya uriccol ilakkaṇac cūttiram.
itaṟku. icai kuṟippup paṇpeṉṉum poruṭkut tāmē yuriyavātaliṉ uriccollāyiṟṟu. perumpāṉmaiyuñ ceyyuṭkuriya vāy varutaliṉ uriccollāyiṟ ṟeṉpāru muḷar eṉṟu cēṉāvaraiyar urai kūṟiyuḷḷār. itaṉāl cēṉāvaraiyar kālattilēyē uriccol ceyyuṭkuriya col eṉṟu oruvarō palarō urai kūṟiyuḷḷatākat terikiṉṟatu.
nālvakaic coṟkaḷil peyar - viṉai, iṭai eṉṟa mūṉṟē ilakkaṇavakaic coṟkaḷākum. uriccolleṉpatu ōr ilakkiyavakaic collēyaṉṟi ilakkaṇavakaic collaṉṟu. atu peyarākavumirukkum. viṉaiyākavumirukkum, iṭaiyākavu mirukkum.
e-ṭu:
peyar	viṉai	iṭai
mālai (iyalpu)	vārtal (nīṭal)	pē - accakkuṟippu
eṟuḻ (vali)	iraṅkal (varuntal)	ai - viyappukkuṟippu
paṇṇai (viḷaiyāṭṭu)	pāytal (parattal).
cēṉāvaraiyarum kaṟuppu, tava veṉpaṉa peyar viṉaippōli, tuvaittal, tuvaikku meṉpaṉa peyar viṉaikku mutaṉilaiyāyiṉa eṉṟu kūṟiyuḷḷār.
cūttirattil, icaiyiṉum kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum tōṉṟal, peyariṉum viṉaiyiṉum meytaṭumāṟal, orucoṟ palaporuṭ kurimaiyum palacol oruporuṭkurimaiyum tōṉṟal, payilātavaṟṟaip payiṉṟavai cārttip poruḷ vēṟu kiḷattal eṉa uriccoṟkuk kūṟiya nāṉkilakkaṇaṅkaḷuḷ mutal mūṉṟum ellāc coṟkum potuvām. iṟutiyoṉṟē uriccoṟkuc ciṟappām. peyarum viṉaiyum iṭaiyum perumpālum icai kuṟippup paṇpiṟ ṟōṉṟiyavaiyē.
e-ṭu:
		icai	kuṟippu	paṇpu
peyar	oli	tuṇi	veḷḷai
viṉai	olittal	tuṇittal	veḷuttal
iṭai	ol	tuṇ 	veḷ
peyariṉum viṉaiyiṉum mey taṭumāṟi eṉṉum pakutikku. peyariṉum viṉaiyiṉum meytaṭumāṟi eṉpatu uriccollākiya urupu peyariṉkaṇṇum viṉaiyiṉkaṇṇum taṭumāṟi e-ṟu. avai taṭumāṟuṅkāl peyar viṉaikaḷaic cārntum. avaṟṟiṟku aṅkamākiyum varum. uṟuvali eṉpatu peyaraic cārntu vantatu. uṟakkoṇṭāṉ eṉpatu viṉaiyaic cārntu vantatu. uṟuvaṉ eṉṟavaḻip peyarkku aṅkamāyiṟṟu, eṉṟu urai varaintār teyvaccilaiyār. itaṉiṉṟum peyar viṉai iṭaikaḷ meytaṭumāṟuvatum eḷitāyuṇarappaṭum. meytaṭumāṟal vaṭivan tirital.
cēvaṭi eṉpatiṟ peyar peyaraic cārntu mey taṭumāṟiṟṟu.
oruppaṭṭāṉ eṉpatiṟ peyar viṉaiyaic cārntu mey taṭumāṟiṟṟu.
kaṇṇaṉ eṉpatiṟ peyar peyarukku aṅkamāyiṟṟu.
(kaṭaik) kaṇittāṉ eṉpatiṟ peyar viṉaikku aṅkamāyiṟṟu.
vārkayiṟu eṉpatil viṉai peyaraic cārntu mey taṭumāṟiṟṟu.
cākkuttiṉāṉ eṉpatil viṉai viṉaiyaic cārntu mey taṭumāṟiṟṟu.
pāṭakaṉ eṉpatil viṉai peyarukku aṅkamāyiṟṟu.
vantāṉ eṉpatil viṉai viṉaikku aṅkamāyiṟṟu.
tillaiccol eṉpatil iṭai peyaraic cārntu mey taṭumāṟiṟṟu.
olla olittatu eṉpatil iṭai viṉaiyaic cārntu mey taṭumāṟiṟṟu.
maṟṟavaṉ eṉpatil iṭai peyarukku aṅkamāyiṟṟu.
eṉṟāṉ eṉpatil iṭai viṉaikku aṅkamāyiṟṟu.
iṉi, orucoṟ palaporuṭ kurimaiyum palacol oru poruṭkurimaiyum ovvōr collilumuḷḷa palaporu ḷorucollum oruporuṭ palacollumākum.
vēḻam eṉpatu karumpu, mūṅkil, yāṉai eṉṟu palaporuḷ paṭum palaporuḷorucoṟ peyar.
varaintāṉ eṉpatu eḻutiṉāṉ, maṇantāṉ, nīkkiṉāṉ eṉṟu pala poruḷpaṭum palaporu ḷorucol viṉai.
koṉ eṉpatu accam, payaṉiṉmai, kālam, perumai eṉap pala poruḷpaṭum palaporu ḷorucol iṭai.
tāvu eṉpatu vali, varuttam eṉap palaporuḷpaṭum palaporu ḷorucol uri.
vēḻam, kaimmalai, ōṅkal eṉpaṉa yāṉaiyaik kuṟikkum oruporuṭ palacoṟ peyar.
nīkkiṉāṉ, akaṟṟiṉāṉ, vilakkiṉāṉ eṉpaṉa oruporuṭ palacol viṉai.
ā, ē, ō eṉpaṉa viṉāp poruḷil varum oruporuṭ palacol iṭai.
cāla, uṟu, tava, naṉi eṉpaṉa mikutip poruḷai yuṇarttum oruporuṭ palacol uri.
oruporuṭ palacol, palaporu ḷorucol eṉpaṉa tiricoṟpākupāṭumākum.
oruporuḷ kuṟitta vēṟucol lākiyum
vēṟuporuḷ kuṟitta orucol lākiyum
irupāṟ ṟeṉpa tiricoṟ kiḷavi   (tol.882).
eṉpatu tolkāppiyam. ākavē mēṟkūṟiya mūṉṟum uriccol lilakkaṇa maṉmai peṟappaṭum.
iṉi uriccol ilakkaṇam yāteṉiṉ,
payilāta vaṟṟai.... poruḷvēṟu kiḷattal   (tol.col.297)
eṉpatēyām. itaṟku,
kēṭpāṉāṟ payilappaṭāta collaip payiṉṟavaṟṟōṭu cārttip peyarum viṉaiyumākiya tattamakkuriya nilaikkaḷat tiṉkaṇ yātāṉumoru collāyiṉum vēṟuvēṟu poru ḷuṇarttappaṭum eṉṟavāṟu eṉṟu cēṉāvaraiyarum.
payilātavaṟṟaip payiṉṟavai cārttit tattam marapiṟ ceṉṟu nilai maruṅkiṉ eṉpatu uriccoṟkaḷ tattam marapiṉāṟ ceṉṟu niṟkumiṭattu. vaḻakkiṉkaṭ payiṉṟu naṭavāta coṟ kaḷaip payiṉṟa coṟkaḷōṭu cērtti e-ṟu. payilāta col lāvaṉa: uṟu, tava, naṉi, payiṉṟa collāvaṉa: mikutal, uṭku, uyarvu eṉpaṉa. cērttutalāvatu iccoṟkaḷ ic colliṉ poruḷpaṭum eṉakkūṭṭutal. eccollāyiṉum poruḷ vēṟu kiḷattal eṉpatu tāṉum oru collāyiṉum poruḷ vēṟupaṭuttik kāṭṭuka e-ṟu eṉṟu teyvaccilaiyārum urai kūṟiyuḷḷaṉar.
itaṉāl uriccoṟkaḷ vaḻakkiṟ payilāta coṟkaḷeṉṟum, avai ceyyuṭkē yuriyaveṉṟum, avaṟṟiṟku iṭam nōkkip poruḷkoḷḷa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, avaṟṟuṭ cila pakutip poruḷ tarāmaṟ piṟaporuḷ tarumeṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
uriccol ceyyuṭ col eṉpatu uṟu, keḻu, mallal, alamaral, maḻavu, kataḻvu, keṭavaral, taṭa, niḻattal, tuvaṉṟu. murañcal, poṟpu. paṇaittal, paiyuḷ, tev, cummai, kaḻum, viḻumam, kamam, kavavu, ilampāṭu, viyal, nām (accam) vaya, tuya, ucā, vayā, nocivu, puṉiṟu, yāṇar, yāṇu, vai (kūrmai), eṟuḻ mutaliya uriccoṟkaḷaik kaṇṭa vaḷavāṉē allatu kēṭṭa vaḷavāṉē aṟiyappaṭum.
uriccoṟkaḷ ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷ āṉamaipaṟṟiya ovvōr uriccollaiyum taṉittaṉi eṭuttuk kūṟip poruḷ kūṟiṉar āciriyar. peyar viṉai iṭaiyeṉṟa maṟṟa mūṉṟu coṟkaṭkum ilakkaṇam maṭṭuṅ kūṟiyavar uriccoṟkut taṉit taṉip poruḷ kūṟic celkiṉṟaṉar. akkālattu akarāti yiṉmaiyāl, ceyyuḷilvarum aruñcoṟkaṭkellām āciriyarē poruḷ kūṟavēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. nikaṇṭukaḷel lām neṭuṅkālattiṟkup piṟappaṭṭavaiyē. mutaṉmuta leḻunta nikaṇṭu tivākaram. piṉpu piṅkalantai, cūṭāmaṇi mutaliya nikaṇṭukaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. tivākarattiṉ kālam ki.pi. āṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu.
tolkāppiyattiṉ kālamō kiṟittuvukkup paṉṉūṟṟāṇṭu muntiyatākum. tolkāppiyak kālattaik ki.mu. pattām nūṟṟāṇṭeṉṟum, nāṉkām nūṟṟāṇṭeṉṟum palarum palavāṟākak kūṟuvar. āyiṉum, kiṟittuvukku muṟpaṭṭateṉpatu maṭṭum tēṟṟamākum.
uriccol ceyyuṭcol eṉpatai aṟintē.
palvakaip paṇpum pakarpeya rāki
orukuṇam palakuṇan taḻuvip peyarviṉai
oruvā ceyyuṭ kuriyaṉa vuriccol   (naṉ.442)
eṉa viḷaṅkak kūṟiṉar pavaṇantiyār.
uri eṉpatu urimai, uriccol eṉpatu oṉṟaṟkuriya col. etaṟkuriya col eṉpatumaṭṭum kuṟippiṟ kūṟiṉarēyaṉṟi veḷippaṭaiyāyk kūṟiṟṟilar tolkāppiyar. atōṭu orucoṟ palaporuṭ kurimai, palacol oruporuṭ kurimai eṉa uriccol lilakkaṇac cūttirattil piṟa coṟṟoṭarkaḷōṭu uri eṉṉum collaip puṇarttatu. ataṉ ciṟappup poruṉ kuṉṟic cilarkkuk kavarpaṭu poruḷ nilaiyākat tōṉṟaṟkuk kāraṇamāyiṟṟu. naṉṉūlil ceyyuṭ kuriyaṉa eṉṉum orē coṟṟoṭaril uri eṉṉum collaip puṇarttu ataṉ ciṟappup poruḷ tōṉṟac ceytār pavaṇantiyār.
icaiyiṉum kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉum tōṉṟi
eṉṟu tolkāppiyattilum,
palvakaip paṇpum pakarpeya rāki   (naṉ.442)
eṉṟu naṉṉūlilum uriyeṉṉum collaip puṇarttātu uriccoṟ poruṭ pākupāṭē allatu uriccol nilaik kaḷap pākupāṭē kūṟiyiruppatāl, icaikuṟippup paṇpeṉ ṉum poruṭku uriya col uriccol eṉṟu cēṉāvaraiyar kūṟuvatu poruntātu. oru coṟ palaporuṭ kurimai, pala col oruporuṭ kurimai eṉṟu tolkāppiyar uriyeṉṟa collaip puṇarttuk kūṟiya coṟṟoṭarkaḷaiyum, oru kuṇam palakuṇan taḻuvi eṉa māṟṟik kūṟiṉār pavaṇantiyār. itaṉāl uriccol ceyyuṭcollē yeṉpatai veḷḷiṭai malaipōl viḷaṅka vaittaṉar.
tolkāppiyam pōṉṟa ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭa uriyiyalē ceyyuḷakarātikaḷāṉa nikaṇṭukaṭkut tōṟṟuvā yākum. ceyyuḷiṟ ciṟappāka vaḻaṅkip poruḷ viḷaṅkāta aruñcoṟkaḷaiyellām taṉittaṉiyeṭuttup poruḷ kūṟiṉar muṉṉai yāciriyar. makkaṭku aṟivāṟṟalum niṉai vāṟṟalum kuṟainta piṟkālattil mikappala ceyyuṭ coṟ kaṭkup poruḷ kūṟavēṇṭi yiruntatiṉālum. avaiyellā vaṟṟaiyum kūṟa ōr ilakkaṇa nūl iṭantarāmaiyāṉum, avaṟṟaik kūṟutaṟkeṉṟē nikaṇṭu eṉṉum nūl eḻuntatu. oru māṇākkaṉ ceyyuḷ vaḻakkāṉa ciṟappuc coṟkaḷaiyel lām muṉṉamē yaṟintāṉāyiṉ piṉpu ceyyum nūlkaḷaik kaṟkumpōtu poruḷuṇarcci eḷitāyirukkum. itaṉāl ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālāṉavaṟṟai māṇākkar maṉappāṭam ceyya vaittaṉar paṇṭaik kaṇakkāyar. itē nōkkattuṭaṉtāṉ nikaṇṭukaḷum eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. makkaṭku iḷamaiyil niṉaivāṟṟal mikkiruppatāl ataip payaṉ paṭuttak karutiyē māṇākkarkku nikaṇṭukaḷai maṉappāṭañ ceyvittaṉar. nikaṇṭukaḷ niṟaivum viḷakkamum paṟṟi varavara nīṇṭu vantaṉa. tivākarattiṉum piṅkalamum, piṅkalattiṉum cūṭāmaṇiyum nīṇṭiruttal kāṇka. nikaṇṭukaḷ ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷaik kūṟuvaṉavēyaṉṟi ulakavaḻakkuc coṟkaḷaik kūṟuvaṉavalla. iṟṟaiya akarātikaḷē iruvakai vaḻakkuc coṟkaḷaiyum eṭuttuk kūṟum. nikaṇṭukaḷ uriccol lakarātikaḷ allatu ceyyuṭcol lakarātikaḷ eṉpatai.
iṉṉa tiṉṉuḻi yiṉṉaṇa miyalum
eṉṟicai nūluṭ kuṇakuṇip peyarkaḷ
collām parattaliṟ piṅkala mutalām
nallō ruriccoli ṉayantaṉar koḷalē   (naṉ.460).
eṉṉum naṉṉūl uriyiyaṟ puṟaṉaṭaic cūttirattāṉu muṇarka. uriccol nikaṇṭu eṉṟē oru nikaṇṭumuḷḷatu. akarāti eṉpatu akaramutaṟ coṟkaḷaik kūṟuvateṉṟē poruḷ paṭiṉum, iṉavilakkaṇamāka nikaṇṭiṟkum peyarākak koḷḷappaṭum.
iṅṅaṉam uriccol ceyyuṭ collāyirukkavum. uric col eṉpatu yātō eṉiṉ, oru vāypāṭṭāṟ collap paṭum poruṭkut tāṉum urittāki varuvatu. ataṉāṉēyaṉṟē orucoṟ palaporuṭkurimai tōṉṟiṉum. palacol oru poruṭ kurimai tōṉṟiṉum eṉa ōtuvārāyiṉar. eḻuttati kārattuḷ itaṉaik kuṟaiccoṟ kiḷavi eṉṟu ōtiṉamaiyāl, vaṭanūlāciriyar tātu eṉṟu kuṟiyiṭṭa coṟkaḷē ivai yeṉṟu koḷḷappaṭum. avaiyum kuṟaiccollātalāṉ. aḵtēl, toḻiṟ poruṇmai uṇarttuvaṉa ellām itaṉuḷ ōtiṉārō eṉiṉ. veḷippaṭu collē kiḷattal vēṇṭā, veḷippaṭa vārā uriccol mēṉa (cū.2) eṉṟārākaliṉ, vaḻakkiṉkaṭ payiṟci illāta coṟkaḷ īṇṭu eṭuttu ōtappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉka.
toḻilāvatu viṉaiyuṅ kuṟippumātaliṉ, av virucoṟkum m§fkh....ṇL¡ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟār teyvaccilaiyār. itaiyē civañāṉa muṉivarun taḻuviṉar.
uriccol tātu (viṉaippakuti) āyiṉ tātuvaik kuṟiyātu alamaral, terumaral, kūrppu, vārtal eṉṟu toḻiṟ peyar kaḷai ēṉ kuṟittal vēṇṭum?
toḻiṟpeyar tamiḻil tātuvaik kuṟikkum vakaiyil oṉṟeṉṟu kūṟiṉ.
kuru, mallal, maḻavu, paṇṇai, vampu, tev, nām, vāḷ, eṟuḻ mutaliya peyarccoṟkaḷ ēṉ kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa? peyarc coṟkaṭkup piratipatikamē yaṉṟit tātuviṉṟē!
iṉi, viṉaiccoṟkaḷuḷḷum pakutipporuḷ oṉṟum, uriccoṟ poruḷoṉṟumā yiruttaliṉ, pakutiyaik kūṟi eṉṉa payaṉ?
cellal eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyar uriccollāki iṉṉā maiyai uṇarttum; cel eṉṉum pakuti pōtalaiyē uṇarttum.
karuvi eṉṉum toḻiṟpeyar uriccollākit tokutiyai uṇarttum; karu eṉṉum pakuti karuttalaiyē uṇarttum.
eyyāmai eṉṉum etirmaṟait toḻiṟpeyar uriccollāki aṟiyāmaiyai uṇarttum; eytal eṉṉum uṭaṉpāṭṭu viṉai ampuviṭuttalaiyē uṇarttum itai.
aṟitaṟ poruṭṭāy eytaleṉṟāṉum, eyttaleṉṟāṉum cāṉṟōr ceyyuṭkaṇ vārāmaiyiṉ, eyyāmai etirmaṟai yaṉmaiyaṟika eṉṉum cēṉāvaraiya ruraiyāṉu maṟika.
ulaka vaḻakkiṉuḷḷum uṭaṉpāṭṭi loṉṟum etir maṟaiyi loṉṟumāka vevvēṟu poruḷpaṭum viṉaikaḷuḷa.
e-ṭu:	uṭaṉpāṭu	etirmaṟai
	poṟumai (patience)	poṟāmai (jealously)
tolkāppiya eḻuttatikāra īṟṟayaṟ cūttirattil,
kuṟippiṉum paṇpiṉu micaiyiṉun tōṉṟi
neṟippaṭa vārāk kuṟaiccoṟ kiḷavi   (tol.eḻuttu. 482).
eṉṟataṉai viṉaippakutiyeṉṟum uriccolleṉṟum kūṟiṉār teyvaccilaiyār. nacciṉārkkiṉiyarum tam uraiyil, kuṟippiṉāṉum paṇpiṉāṉum icaiyiṉāṉum piṟantu, oru vaḻippaṭa vārāta coṟṟaṉmai kuṟainta coṟkaḷākiya uric coṟkaḷum eṉṟē muṟkūṟiṉārēṉum.
kaṇ viṇṇa viṇaittatu, viṇ viṇaittatu. ivai kuṟippuric col; āṭai veḷḷa viḷarttatu. veḷviḷarttatu. ivai paṇpuriccol; kaṭal olla olittatu, ollolittatu. ivai icaiyuriccol. olloli nīr pāyvatē pōlun tuṟaivaṉ eṉṟār ceyyuṭkaṇṇum. ivai uyirīṟāyum puḷḷiyīṟāyum niṟṟaliṉ oṉṟaṉkaṇ aṭakka lākāmaiyiṉ neṟippaṭa vārā eṉṟār. viṇṇa viṇaittatu teṟipput tōṉṟat teṟitta teṉṟum, viṇviṇaittatu teṟipput teṟittateṉṟum ām. ēṉaiyavaṟṟiṟkum ivvāṟē uṇarka. iṅṅaṉam niṟṟaliṟ ṟaṉmai kuṟainta collāyiṟṟu. viṉaiyē kuṟippē (col. 258) eṉṉuñ cūttirattiṟ kūṟiya eṉa eṉpataṉai ivaṟṟōṭu kūṭṭiyavaḻi iṭaiccollātaliṉ viṇṇeṉa viṇaittatu eṉap puṇarkkappaṭumāṟu uṇarka eṉṟu piṟkūṟiyatiliruntu, cūttirattiṟ kuṟaiccoṟ kiḷaviyeṉak kuṟittatu iṭaic collē yeṉṟum, viṇṇa, veḷḷa eṉṟu kuṟaiyāy niṟṟaliṉ kuṟaiccoṟ kiḷaviyeṉappaṭṭateṉṟum aṟintu koḷḷalām.
tolkāppiyar kuṟaic coṟkiḷavi eṉṉum peyarāl viṉaip pakutiyaiyē kuṟittiruppārāyiṉ, eccaviyalil, mutaṟkuṟai, iṭaikkuṟai, kaṭaikkuṟai eṉṉum mūvakaik kuṟaikaḷaik kuṟikkumiṭattu.
kuṟaiccoṟ kiḷavi kuṟaikkumvaḻi yaṟital
eṉac cūttiram ceytirār.
kuṟaittaṉa vāyiṉu niṟaippeya riyala
eṉṉum aṭutta cūttirattiṉāl mūvakaik kuṟaikaḷum peyarc coṟkaḷē yeṉpatu peṟappaṭum. ākaiyāl, kuṟaiccol eṉpatu kuṟainta coṟkaṭkellām potuppeyarēyaṉṟi viṉaippakutikkē yuriya ciṟappuk kuṟiyīṭu aṉṟeṉpatu terintukoḷka.
uriccolleṉpatu viṉaippakutiyēyāyiṉ, vēṟṟumai mayaṅkiyaluḷ,
viṉaiyē ceyvatu eṉṉum cūttirattil viṉai eṉpataṉālum, eccaviyaluḷ.
ceyyā yeṉṉu muṉṉilai viṉaiccol
ceyyeṉ kiḷavi yākiṭa ṉuṭaittē   (tol.col.593)
eṉṉuñ cūttirattāṉum viṉaippakutiyait tolkāppiyar kuṟittiruttaliṉ, atai mīṇṭum uriyiyal eṉṟōriyaliṟ kūṟuvatu kūṟiyatu kūṟalāmeṉka. iṉi,
veḷippaṭu collē kiḷattal vēṇṭā
veḷippaṭa vārā vuriccoṉ mēṉa.   (tol.col.783)
eṉṟu tolkāppiyar uriccollai veḷippaṭu col, veḷippaṭāc col eṉa iruvakaippaṭuttiyiruppatāl. uriccol veḷippaṭāc collākavēyirukkiṉṟa ceyyuṭ collaṉṟu eṉak kūṟuvar cilar.
ulakattiṟ pala vakuppārukkuḷḷum pala kuḻūukkuṟikaḷ vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟaṉa. oru kuḻuviṉaip piṟarkkut teriyāvaṇṇam maṟai poruḷaṉavākat tamakkuḷ vaḻaṅkikkoḷḷum kuṟikaḷē kuḻūukkuṟiyākum. āyiṉum nāḷaṭaivil cila kuḻūuk kuṟi kaḷiṉ poruḷkaḷ piṟarkku veḷiyākiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. aṅṅaṉam poruḷ veḷiyāṉa kuḻūukkuṟikaḷ uṇmaiyil kuḻūukkuṟi yeṉṟē kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kaḷḷaik kuṟikkum kutirai yeṉṉuñ collum, poṉṉaik kuṟikkum paṟiyeṉṉuñ collum ellārāṉum poruḷaṟiyappaṭiṉum iṉṉum kuḻūukkuṟi yākavē kūṟappaṭum. atupōlac ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷāṉa uric coṟkaḷum poruḷaṟiyappaṭṭa piṉṉum uriccolleṉavē paṭum. āṉāl, ulaka vaḻakkiṟku vantavaimaṭṭum uriccol lākā.
coṟkaḷ vaḻakku mikutipaṟṟip poruḷaṟiyappaṭuvatum, vaḻak kiṉmaipaṟṟip poruḷaṟiyappaṭāmaiyumuṇṭu. vaḻakkuk kālantōṟum māṟikkoṇṭēyirukkum.
tolkāppiyar tam kālattil ulakavaḻakkil vaḻaṅkātaṉavākak kuṟikkiṉṟa pacappu, atirvu, tīrtal, paḻutu, muḻutu, ceḻumai, cēr mutaliya coṟkaḷ ikkālattup peruvaḻakkāka vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. tolkāppiyar kālattil ivai vaḻaṅkiyiruppiṉ ivaṟṟukkup poruḷ kūṟiyirār. iṉit tam kālattil putitāy vaḻaṅkiṉavāka avar kūṟum kaṭuvaṉ. kōṭṭāṉ, tattai, pūcai mutaliya ēḻu coṟkaḷil, kōṭṭāṉ, pūcai eṉṉumiraṇṭē ikkālattu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ēṉaiya vaḻakkiṟantaṉa. pūcai eṉpatu ik kālattup pūṉai eṉa vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu.
ākaiyāl, tolkāppiyar kūṟiya uriccoṟkaḷellām akkālattuc ceyyuṭ coṟkaḷāyiruntaṉa veṉṟum, avaṟṟuṭ cila ikkālattu ulaka vaḻakkil vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, avai ikkālattu uriccollākāveṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
iṉi, uriccolleṉpatu ellōrkkum poruḷviḷaṅkāta ceyyuṭ collāyiṉ, tiricol eṉpatum ellōrkkum poruḷ viḷaṅkāta ceyyuṭ collāyiruttaliṉ uriccol lāmō veṉiṉ, kūṟutum:
iyaṟcol, tiricol eṉpaṉa iyalpāyp poruḷuṇarttum col. iyaṟcoṟ ṟaṉmaiyiṉiṉṟun tirintu aritāyp poruḷuṇarttum col eṉap potuvāyp kūṟappaṭiṉum, coṟporuḷ nōkkiṉ iyalpāṉa col (Primitive word), iyalpāṉa colliṉiṉṟum tirinta col (Derivative) eṉṟē kūṟaṟkēṟṟaṉa. itu tiricoṟ cūttirattiṟku.
1. tiricollāvatu uṟupputtiritalum muḻuvatum tiritalumeṉa iru vakaittu. kiḷḷai, maññai eṉpa uṟupputtirintaṉa. vilaṅkal, viṇṭu eṉpaṉa muḻuvatun tirintaṉa.... tiripuṭaimaiyē tiricoṟkilakkaṇamātal colliṉ muṭivi ṉapporuṇ muṭittal eṉpataṉāṟ peṟavaittār eṉṟu cēṉāvaraiyar kūṟiya uraiyāṉum, itaiyoṭṭi nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟiya uraiyāṉum uṇarappaṭum.
uriccol ceyyuṭ collākavē yiruttalāṉum, ōr ilakkaṇa vakaic collaṉmaiyāṉum, peyar, viṉai, iṭai eṉṉum muccollāyu miruttalāṉum, pirayōkampaṟṟiyē iṉṉa colleṉak kūṟappaṭuvatākum. tolkāppiyar eccaviyaluḷ.
iṭaiccollellām piṟitōr collai vicēṭikkum kollē eṉṉum poruḷil.
iṭaiccol lellām, vēṟṟumaic collē
eṉṟu cūttirattu. ipporuḷai uriccollum orupuṭai taḻuvumpaṭi ataiyaṭuttu.
uriccoṉ maruṅkiṉu muriyavai  yuriya
eṉṟu kūṟiṉār. itaṟkuc cēṉāvaraiyar.
uriccolliṭattum vēṟupaṭukkuñ collātaṟkum uriyaṉa uriyavām: ellām uriyavākā eṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē, uriccol luḷ vēṟupaṭuttum vēṟupaṭukkappaṭṭum irunilaimaiyu muṭaiyavāy varuvaṉavē perumpāṉmai yeṉpatām.
vēṟupaṭukkuñ collē yāvaṉa uṟu, tava, naṉi eṉṉuñ toṭakkattaṉa. irunilaimaiyumuṭaiyaṉa kuru. keḻu, cellal, iṉṉal eṉṉuñ toṭakkattaṉa. uṟuporuḷ, tavappala, naṉicēyttu, ēkallaṭukkam eṉa ivai oṉṟai vicēṭittallatu vārāmaiyum, kurumaṇi, viḷaṅkukuru, kēḻkiḷarakalam, ceṅkēḻ: cellaṉōy, aruñcellal, iṉṉaṟ kuṟippu. peyariṉṉal eṉa ivai oṉṟaṉai vicēṭittum vicēṭikkappaṭṭum iru nilaimaiyuṭaiyavāy varumāṟum. vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷu nōkkik kaṇṭukoḷka. kuruviḷaṅkiṟṟu; cellaṟīra eṉat tāmē niṉṟu viṉai koḷvaṉa, vicēṭikkappaṭun taṉmai yuṭaiyavātaliṉ, vicēṭikkappaṭuñ collām. piṟavum vicēṭittallatu vārātaṉavum, vicēṭittum vicēṭiyātum varuvaṉavum, vaḻakkuñ ceyyuḷu nōkkiyuṇarka eṉṟu urai kūṟiṉar.
itaṉāl, uriccol eṉpatu peyarākavum viṉaiyākavum. avaṟṟiṟku aṭaiyākavum varumeṉṟum, peyarukku aṭai yāṅkāl kuṟippup peyareccam pōlvateṉṟum, viṉaikku aṭaiyāṅkāl kuṟippu viṉaiyeccam pōlvateṉṟum, peyaruriccol viṉaiyuric col eṉṉum iru vakaiyuḷ ellā vuriccollum aṭaṅkāveṉṟum aṟintukoḷka.
itukāṟuṅ kūṟiyavāṟṟāl uriccol eṉpatu ceyyuṭ collē yeṉṟum. ataṟkup piṟavāṟu kūṟalellām pōli yuraiyeṉṟum. uriccol ōr ilakkaṇavakait taṉiccol laṉṟeṉṟum, atu pirayōkattiṟkēṟpa iṉṉa colleṉṟu kūṟappaṭumeṉṟum teṟṟeṉat terintukoḷka. ( centamiḻc celvi kaṭakam 1935)
urimaippēṟu
oruvarkku uriyatu urimai. atu puṟavuṭal paṟṟiyatum akavuṭal paṟṟiyatum āka irutiṟappaṭum. muṉṉatu ellārkkum potu: piṉṉatu paṇpaṭṭa makkaṭkē ciṟappu. māntarkku ovvoru nilaiyilum ovvoru allatu ciṟcila urimaikaḷ tēvai. avai nīrvēṭkaiyum uṇavu vēṭkaiyum pōṉṟavai. avaṟṟai muyaṉṟō, piṟarai vēṇṭiyō, avarōṭu pōrāṭiyō, peṟal vēṇṭum.
irutiṟa vurimaikaḷum piṉvarumāṟu aivakaippaṭum.
1. vaḷarvurimai
kaṇavaṉ maṉaiviyariṉ iṉpak kūṭṭattōṭu piḷḷaippēṟu toṭarpupaṭuttappaṭṭiruppatāl, peṟṟōr virumpiṉum virumpāviṭiṉum perumpālum piḷḷaikaḷ piṟantuviṭu kiṉṟaṉa. nilattil vitaitta vitai muḷaittal pōṉṟē piḷḷaippēṟum iruppatāl,
kēḷātē vantu kiḷaikaḷā yiṟṟōṉṟi   (30)
eṉṟu nālaṭiyār kūṟuvatu poruntātu. piḷḷaikaḷ peṟ ṟōr viṉaiyālēyē peṟappaṭuvatāl, avaṟṟai vaḷarppatu avar kaṭamaiyum, avarāl vaḷarkkappaṭuvatu avaṟṟiṉ urimaiyum, ākiṉṟatu.
emmaik kēḷātu ēṉ piṟantāy?
eṉṟu, peṟṟōr tam piḷḷai vaḷarppait taṭṭik kaḻittuviṭa muṭiyātu.
kuḻavi piṟantavuṭaṉ uṇaviṟku aḻuvatē, ōr urimai vēṭkai yum ataip peṟum muyaṟciyum ākiṉṟatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil tāy vīṭṭu vēlaiyil āḻntirukkumpōtu, uriya vēḷaiyil taṉ cēykkup pāl koṭātiruppatum uṇṭu. avvēḷaiyil kuḻantaiyiṉ aḻukaiyoli kēṭṭut tāy pāl koṭuppatu vaḻakkam. ataṉāl, aḻutapiḷḷai pāl kuṭikkum eṉṉum paḻamoḻiyum eḻuntatu.
kuḻavip paruvattilum kuḻantaip paruvattilum piḷḷaip paruvattilum vaḷarppatum, paiyaṟ paruvattilum iḷamaṭṭap paruvattilum uṇavaḷittuk kāppatōṭu ōr aluvaṟkō toḻilukkō payiṟṟuvippatum, paruvam vantapiṉ maṇañ ceytu vaippatum, peṟṟōriṉ kaṭamaiyām. annaṉmai kaḷaip peṟuvatu piḷḷaikaḷiṉ urimaiyām.
kaittoḻiṟ payiṟciyiṉuṅ kalvip payiṟci ciṟantatātalālum, iruvakaip payiṟciyum oruṅkē peṟutaluṅ kūṭumātalālum,
tantai makaṟkāṟṟum naṉṟi avaiyattu
munti yiruppac ceyal.  (kuṟaḷ.67)
īṉṟa poḻutiṟ perituvakkum taṉmakaṉaic
cāṉṟōṉ eṉakkēṭṭa tāy   (kuṟaḷ.69)
eṉṟu tiruvāymalarntār tiruvaḷḷuvar.
2. vāḻvurimai
oruvar ivvulakil vāḻvataṟkuk kalvip payiṟci allatu toḻiṟ payiṟci, vēlaippēṟu, pātukāppu - im mūṉṟum iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa. oru kuṭumpattiṟ piṟantavaṉukku eṅṅaṉam vaḷarvurimaiyuṇṭō, aṅṅaṉamē oru nāṭṭiṟ piṟantavaṉukku vāḻvurimaiyuṇṭu. ataṟkuriya mūṉṟaiyum aḷikkak kūṭiyatum kaṭamaippaṭṭuḷḷatum, āḷum aracē.
payiṟciyiṉṟi evvēlaiyum peṟavum ceyyavum iyalātākai yāl, nāṭṭiṟ piṟantavarkkellām. avaravar tiṟamai,viruppam, uṭaṟkūṟu, maṉappāṉmai, mativaṉmai mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkut takkavāṟu, vevvēṟu tuṟaiyiṟ payiṟciyaḷittal vēṇṭum. eṇṇum eḻuttum kaṇṇeṉat takum. ātalāl, mutaṟkaṇ, nālakavaikku mēṟpaṭṭa ellāp piḷḷai kaṭkum ilavacak kaṭṭāyat tuvakkak kalviyaḷittal vēṇṭum. enta amaippum nilaiyākavum uṟutiyākavum iruttaṟku aṭippaṭai akaṉ ṟiruttal vēṇṭum. iṉṟēl, mēṟpaṭai kulaintupōm.
viḷaiyum payir muḷaiyil teriyum. ātalāl, tuvakkap paḷḷik kalvip paruvattilēyē, peṟṟōrum āciriyarum piḷḷaikaḷiṉ tiṟamaiyaiyum maṉappāṉmaiyaiyum kūrntu nōkkiyaṟintu, akakkaraṇa valimai viñciyavarai mēṟkalvip payiṟcikkum, puṟakkaraṇa valimai viñciyavarait toḻiṟ payiṟcikkum, aṉupputal vēṇṭum.
mēṟkalvip payiṟciyilum, aṉpum toṇṭu maṉappāṉmaiyum uḷḷavarai maruttuvak kalvikkum nāvaṉmaiyum niṉaivāṟṟa lum naṭunilaiyum uḷḷavaraic caṭṭak kalvikkum, ōviya varaivāṟṟalum putuppuṉaivut tiṟaṉum kaṇita matiyum uḷḷa varaip poṟiviṉai allatu cūḻcciyaviṉaik kalvikkum. iṭaiviṭāp paṭippum eṭuttu viḷakkun tiṟaṉum uḷḷavarai āciriyap payiṟcik kalvikkum, vaḷarttiyum uṭal valimaiyum añcāmaiyum tuṇiceyal maṟamum uḷḷavaraip paṭait tuṟaik kalvikkum, iṉkuralum icaiviruppum nīḷviralum uḷḷavarai icaikkalvikkum, teyvappattiyum tūyavoḻukka mum maṟainūl viruppum uḷḷavarai mataviyaṟ kalvikkum, ivvāṟē piṟa tuṟaikaṭkum terinteṭuttal vēṇṭum.
kallūrikaḷil iṭamillāviṭiṉ, putukkallūrikaḷait tōṟṟu vittal vēṇṭum. kāraik kaṭṭaṭam illāviṭiṉ kūraik kaṭṭaṭattilēṉum toṭaṅkutal vēṇṭum.
kaṇitam vēṇṭiyavaṉai varalāṟṟu vakuppilum, maruttuvap payiṟci virumpupavaṉaic caṭṭak kallūriyilum, iṅṅaṉamē ēṉait tuṟaikaḷilum tāṟumāṟākac cērppatu, vaṇṭiyiṟ kaṭṭavēṇṭiya kāḷaiyai vīṭṭuk kāvaṟkum, vīṭṭuk kāvaṟ kuriya nāyai iṟaivaiyēṟṟattiṟkum, poti cumakka vēṇṭiya kaḻutaiyai ūrvalattiṟkum, ūrvalattiṟkuriya yāṉaiyai narakal vaṇṭikkum, vaippatum kaṭṭuvatum amarttuvatum pōṉṟatē.
toḻiṟpayiṟciyāyiṉum mēṟkalvip payiṟciyāyiṉum, payiṟci peṟṟa ellārum vēlaikkuriyar. palarukku vēlaiyillai yeṉṟu kaivirippatu poṟuppāṭciyaṉṟu. makkaḷtokai mikkiruppiṉ, ataip palvēṟu vaḻikaḷil maṭṭuppaṭuttalām. āyiṉ.
irantum uyirvāḻtal vēṇṭiṉ parantu
keṭuka ulakiyaṟṟi yāṉ   (kuṟaḷ.1062)
eṉṟu tiruvaḷḷuvar iṟaivaṉ mītē vacai pāṭiyiruttalai nōkkuka.
makkaḷtokai maṭṭiṟku miñciya ikkālattil, taṉiyuṭaimai yāṭcikku iṭamiṉṟēl, aḷavuṭaimaiyāṭciyum. atuvum pōtāteṉiṉ kūṭṭuṭaimaiyāṭciyum, iṉṟiyamaiyātaṉa vām. oru nāṭṭiṟ piṟantu vēlaip payiṟci peṟṟa ellārkkum kulamatak kaṭci vēṟupāṭiṉṟit tiṟamaikkēṟṟavāṟu vēlai yaḷippatē kūṭṭuṭaimaiyāṭciyām. atuvē iraciyāvilum cīṉāvilum iṉṟu iruntuvaruvatu. ataip potuvuṭaimai yāṭciyeṉpatu tavaṟu. potuvuṭamaiyāṭci paṇamiṉṟik kuṭumpa vāḻkkaipōl naṭappatu. atu oru nāṭṭiṟku ēṟkātu.
pakuttuṇṭu palluyir ōmputal nūlōr
tokuttavaṟṟuḷ ellān talai   (kuṟaḷ.302)
eṉṟu akkālattilēyē tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟiyiruttal kāṇka.
aḷavuṭaimaiyāṭci yeṉiṉum varampuṭaimaiyāṭci yeṉi ṉum okkum.
iṉi, kuruṭar muṭavar mutaliyōr aiyam (piccai) peṟuvatum, vaṟiya nāṭukaḷ celva nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu utavi peṟuvatum vāḻvurimaiyiṉpāṟ paṭuvatē.
3. viṭutalaiyurimai
taṉippaṭṭa aṭimai taṉ talaivaṉiṭattiṉiṉṟum, paṇṇai yāḷkaḷ tam āṇṭaiyiṉiṉṟum, oru kulattār iṉṉoru kulattāriṉiṉṟum, oru nāṭṭār iṉṉoru nāṭṭāri ṉiṉṟum, viṭutalai peṟuvatu viṭutalaiyurimaiyām.
āṭavarpōṟ kalvi peṟuvatum tērtalkaḷiṟ kuṭavōlaiyiṭu vatum, aluvalakaṅkaḷil āṭavaruṭaṉ vēlai ceyvatum, kaṇavaṉ iṟantapiṉ kaimmai (kaimpeṇ) vāḻvu vāḻvatum maṟumaṇam ceytu koḷvatum, peṇṭir peṟa vēṇṭiya urimaikaḷām.
kulak kaṭṭuppāṭum peṟṟōr taṭaiyum mīṟic ciṟanta muṟaiyil ulakam pōṟṟumāṟu kūṭi vāḻvatu, maṇamakkaḷ urimaiyām.
iṉi, kaṭcit talaivarkkuriyatu pēccurimai: eḻuttāḷarukkum nūlāciriyarukkum uriyatu eḻutturimai: oruvaṉ taṉ tāy moḻiyiṟ pēca iṭamiruppatu moḻiyurimai; oru vakuppār oruvakai uṇavaiyuṇṇac caṭṭat taṭaiyillā tiruppatu uṇavurimai.
iṉi, tamiḻ tirāviṭattiṟkut tāyum āriyattiṟku mūlamumāṉa ulaka mutal uyartaṉic cemmoḻiyātalāl aṉaittintiya āṭci moḻiyākat tamiḻum amaivatu tamiḻariṉ moḻi yurimaiyām.
4.potunalavurimai
taṉi vāḻkkaiyil oruvaṉ evvaḷavum īṭṭalām; eṅkuñ cellalām. āyiṉ, kūṭṭu vāḻkkaiyil, potunalampaṟṟip paṟpala kaṭṭuppāṭukaḷum varampīṭukaḷum vakukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avai taṉivāḻkkaiyil urimait taṭaiyēṉum, kūṭṭu vāḻkkaiyil urimaiyēyām.
iru piḷḷaikaṭkumēṟ peṟakkūṭāteṉpatu piḷḷai varampīṭu: patiṉaintu ceykaṭkumēl nilam vaittirukkak kūṭā teṉpatu, celva varampīṭṭil oruvakaiyāṉa nilavarampīṭu. ivaiyellām, oru mānakariṟ pakal vēḷaiyil pōkkuvarattu mikka iṭattil, oruvaṉ oru cālaiyiṉ kuṟukkē cellak kūṭāteṉpatu pōṉṟavaiyē.
5. matavurimai
ulakiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa poruḷkaḷaip paṟṟiyum karuttu vēṟu pāṭirukkumāṟu. makkaḷ maṉappāṉmai paltiṟap paṭṭuḷḷatu. ākavē, kāṇappaṭāta kaṭavuḷaip paṟṟiyum maṟumaiyaip paṟṟiyum karuttu vēṟu pāṭṭiṟku ettuṇai yiṭamuṇṭeṉ pataic collavē vēṇṭuvatillai.
matanampikkaiyellām ēraṇa muṟaikkum aṟiviyal meyppip piṟkum aṭaṅkātu. appāṟpaṭṭu, ōraḷavu kuruṭṭuttaṉa mākavō kuraṅkup piṭiyākavō iruppiṉum, uyirvāḻvai yum poruṭpaṭuttāta aḷaviṟku makkaḷ maṉattai iṟukap piṇikkum ceytikaḷuḷ talaiciṟantatu matamātalāl, paṇpāṭu mikka ceṅkōlaracellām, tam kuṭikaṭku matavurimai yaḷittēyuḷḷaṉa.
nam intiyak kuṭiyaracu paṉṉāṭṭuk kūṭṭāṭciyātalāl, matavurimai yaḷittiruppatu mikat takkatē.
iṉi, civamatamum tirumāl matamum teṉṉāṭṭut tūya tamiḻ mataṅkaḷātalāl, iṉimēl tamiḻnāṭṭut tirukkōvilkaḷil, payiṟci peṟṟa palvakait tamiḻ vakuppārē, tamiḻil maṭṭum vaḻipāṭāṟṟutal vēṇṭum. itu tamiḻar matavurimaiyākum.
pirāmaṇar veḷināṭṭiṉiṉṟu vantēṟikaḷiṉ vaḻiyiṉarā talālum, ciṟupāṉmaiyarātalālum, tamiḻaiyē tāymoḻi yākak koṇṭiruppatālum. āriyam tiraviṭattiṉ tiripātalā lum, āriya mantiraṅkaḷellām tamiḻar aṟiyāta ciṟuteyvak kolaivēḷvi vaḻipāṭu paṟṟiyaṉavātalālum, camaṟkirutam vētamoḻiyum tamiḻuṅ kalanta ilakkiya moḻiyātalālum, āriyamoḻi tēvamoḻiyeṉṟum pirāmaṇar nilattēvareṉ ṟum ēmāṟṟappaṭṭataṉālēyē, iṭaikkālat tamiḻar pirāmaṇar āriya mantiraṅkaḷaik koṇṭu tirukkōvil vaḻipāṭu ceyya iṭantantaṉarā talālum. av vēmāṟṟu iṉṉun toṭara immiyun takuti yillai eṉa aṟika.
pirāmaṇar tamiḻukku uṇmaiyāyiruntu tamiḻilēyē vaḻipāṭu naṭattiṉ, avar pūcait toḻilait toṭarntu ceyyalām. itukāṟum uyartiṇaiyāṉa makkaḷiṉ palvēṟu urimaikaḷ kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉi, vāyillā ēḻai aḵṟiṇai uyiriṉaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṉ urimaiyai nām namatupōṟ pēṇa vēṇṭāvā? ataiyum pēṇāviṭiṉ, nām uyartiṇaiyeṉṟu tarukkuvatu eṅṅaṉ takum?
māṭukaḷum, kutiraikaḷum, ēruḻavum nīriṟaikkavum vaṇṭiyiḻuk kavum, iravum pakalum vaṟpuṟuttappaṭṭu, pōtiya vuṇaviṉṟiyum muraṭṭut taṉamāka aṭiyuṇṭum kuttuṇṭum, puṇmēṟ puṇpaṭṭum ettuṇait tuṉpappaṭukiṉṟaṉa! avai eṅṅaṉ yāriṭam muṟaiyiṭum? eṅṅaṉam mānāṭu kūṭum? eṅṅaṉam kūṭṭaṇi cērum? eṅṅaṉam vēlai niṟuttañ ceyyum? uṇṇānōṉpiṉālum vaṉceyalālum tam tollai kaḷaiyum tuṉpaṅkaḷaiyum terivikkavō tīrttuk koḷḷavō immiyum avaṟṟiṟku iṭamillaiyē!
makkaḷiṭattu aṉpu ceyya vēṇṭumeṉṟum, maṟṟa vuyirka ḷiṭattu aruḷpūṇṭoḻuka vēṇṭumeṉṟum, āyirakkaṇak kāṉa paṉuvalkaḷum nammiṭattuḷḷaṉa, iruntum eṉṉa payaṉ?
amerikkarum mēṉāṭṭārum nampōla naṭikkāviṭiṉum, poṟiviṉaip puraṭciyai uṇṭupaṇṇi ellā viṉaikaḷai yum cūḻcciyat tuṇaikoṇṭē ceytuvaruvatāl, kutiraiyiṉa mum kaḻutaiyiṉamum muṟṟum viṭutalai peṟṟuḷḷaṉa. māṭ ṭiṉam uṇaviṟkaṉṟi vēṟev vakaiyiluṅ kollappaṭuva tillai.
nāmum mēlaiyarpōṟ poṟiviṉaip puraṭciyai yuṇṭākki, mēṟkūṟiya vilaṅkiṉaṅkaḷiṉ urimaiyaik kāppōmāka. (urimai vēṭkai poṅkal malar 1973.)
urimai vaḻi
makkaḷukkut talaimuṟai talaimuṟaiyāy urimai toṭarntu varumvaḻi tantaivaḻi tāyvaḻi allatu makaṉvaḻi makaḷvaḻi eṉa iraṇṭām. ivaṟṟuḷ tāyvaḻiyē paḻantamiḻ nāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vanta teṉpataṟkut tāyam eṉṉum peyarē cāṉṟām. tāyvaḻip peṟuvatu tāyam.
tantaivaḻi allatu makaṉvaḻi āṇvaḻiyum, tāyvaḻi allatu makaḷ vaḻi peṇ vaḻiyum ākum. āttirēliyā eṉṉum teṉ kaṇṭattil peṇvaḻi urimaiyē iruntu varuvatākac collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
makaḷ peṟuvatu marumakaṉukkē uriyatu ātalāl makaḷvaḻit tāyam marumakkaḷ tāyam eṉappaṭum. oruvaṉatu cottai avaṉuṭaiya uṭaṉpiṟantāḷiṉ āṇmakkaḷ aṭaivateṉac collappaṭum marumakkaḷ tāyam malaiyāḷa nāṭṭil iṉṟum vaḻaṅki varukiṉṟatu. cōḻa pāṇṭiya nāṭukaḷil makkaḷ tāyam vaḻaṅkivara cēra nāṭākiya malaiyāḷattil maṭṭum marumakkaḷ tāyam vaḻaṅki varakkāraṇam annāṭu kuṭamalaiyāl pirikkap paṭṭiruppatum, neṭuṅkālamāka ēṉaiyiru tamiḻnāṭu kaḷuṭaṉum toṭarpu koḷḷāmaiyumē.
marumakkaḷ tāyamē paṇṭait tamiḻ nāṭṭil vaḻaṅki vantamai yāl vaḻittōṉṟal marukaṉ eṉappaṭṭāṉ. ippeyar marumakaṉ eṉpataṉ marūu.  (col.21,22.)

vaṭamoḻiyiluḷḷa coṟkaḷellām vaṭacollēyeṉṟu iṭaik kālattil oru tavaṟāṉa karuttu makkaḷ maṉattil vērūṉṟiyiruntatiṉāl, arattam eṉṉum teṉcolliṉ vaṭacol vaṭivāṉa rakta eṉpataiyē mūlamākak koṇṭu, atait tamiḻ muṟaikkēṟpa ikaram muṉṉiṭṭu irattam eṉac collavum eḻutavum talaippaṭṭaṉar. itu, araṅkaṉ eṉṉum teṉcollai raṅkaṉ eṉṉum vaṭacol vaṭiviṉ tiripākak koṇṭu, iraṅkaṉ eṉṟu tamiḻil tavaṟāka eḻutuva tottatē. âthfu« (8m« ü‰wh©L), ã§fy« (10M« ü‰wh©L?), cūṭāmaṇi (16ām nūṟṟāṇṭu) mutaliya coṟṟokutikaḷ (nikaṇṭukaḷ) iṭaikkālattaṉavātaliṉ, avaṟṟuḷḷum irattam eṉṉum vaṭivu iṭam peṟṟuḷḷatu. itu piṟkāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷ avvaṭivai yāḷa iṭan tantuviṭṭatu. āyiṉum, itu moḻinūlaṟivārāycci mikka kālamātalālum, ilakkiya vaḻuvaik kaḷaitaṟku oru kāla varampiṉmaiyāṉum, iṉi, taṉiccollāyiṉum toṭarc collāyiṉum irattam eṉṉum vaṭivai arattam eṉṟē tirutti āḷutal takkatām. arattam eṉpatai nilaic collākak koṇṭu iruvakai vaḻakkilum perumpāṉmaiyāka varum toṭarc coṟkaḷ, arattakkaṭṭi, arattakkaṭṭu, arattak kaṇṇaṉ, arattak kalappu, arattak kaviccu, arattak kaḻiccal, arattak kaṟai, arattak kaṉappu, arattak kāṭṭēṟi, arattak kāṇikkai, arattak kuḻal, arattak kuḻāy, arattak kuṟaiccal, arattak kotippu, arattak koḻuppu, arattak kōmāri, arattak kumutam, arattak kuṉmam, arattac civappu, arattac  curappu, arattac curuṭṭai, arattac cūṟai, arattap pali, arattap paḻi, arattap piṇṭam, arattap pittam, arattap puṭaiyaṉ, arattap perukku, arattap pōḷam, aratta maṭakki, aratta maṇṭalam, aratta maṇṭali, aratta māṭaṉ, aratta māṉiyam, aratta muṭi, aratta mūttiram, aratta mūlam, aratta vaṭi, aratta valippu, aratta vaḻalai, aratta vaḷḷi, aratta vintu, aratta viriyaṉ, aratta vuṟavu, aratta veṭṭai, aratta veṟi, aratta vērvai, aratta vōṭṭam eṉpaṉa.

neruppu civanta poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. ataṉāl neruppu niṟam eṉpatu cenniṟattaik kuṟikkum.
civanta poruṭ peyarkaḷē paṇpiyāku peyarākac cen niṟattai uṇarttuvatumuṇṭu.
e-ṭu: pavaḻavāy = civanta utaṭu.
kurutik kāntaḷ = ceṅkāntaṉ.
immuṟaiyilēyē, arakku, arattam eṉṉum peyarkaḷum cenniṟattai yuṇarttum.
alattam, arattam eṉṉum coṟkaṭku mūlamāṉa al, ar eṉpaṉavum; ilantai ilatti eṉṉum coṟkaṭku mūlamāṉa il, ir eṉpaṉavum; elli, eri, eṉṉum coṟkaṭku mūlamāṉa el, er eṉpaṉavum; oṉṟōṭoṉṟu toṭarpuṭaiyaṉavāy, ul eṉṉum aṭi vēriṉiṉṟu tōṉṟi, neruppaiyō, nerup piṉ niṟattaiyō, anniṟamuḷḷa poruḷkaḷaiyō, uṇarttu meṉpatum; arattam arakku, alattakam eṉṉum coṟkaḷ ilantai, iratti eṉpaṉa pōṉṟē tūya teṉcoṟkaḷ eṉpatum; teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
arakku eṉpatu civappu niṟattaiyum, arakkam eṉpatu arakku, arattam mutaliya cenniṟap poruḷkaḷaiyum kuṟippataṉālum; amerikkap paḻaṅkuṭi makkaḷuḷ oru cārār cevvintiyar eṉappaṭuvatālum; arakkar eṉṉum vakuppār paḻaṅkālattiṉa rākat tolkataikaḷ kūṟuvataṉālum, arakkaṉ eṉṉum peyar cenniṟam paṟṟiya arakku eṉṉuñ colliṉiṉṟu tirintiruppa tāka aṟiñar cilar karutiṉar. aḵtu arakku eṉṉum viṉaic colliṉiṉṟu tirintatākum. arakkutal = varuttutal, aḻittal. ellarakkum ayil nutivē lirāvaṇaṉum (kamparā. ūrtēṭu. 230). arakkukiṉṟavaṉ arakkaṉ; araṭṭukiṉṟavaṉ araṭṭaṉ eṉpatupōl. arakkarellām makkaḷai varuttiṉavarākavē kūṟappaṭuvar. arakkaṉ eṉpatu mīṭṭal (iraṭcittal) eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum. rakṣa eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉiṉṟu tirintataṉṟu. kuṭikaḷ aracaṉukku aṭaṅki vari koṭuppatāl, ulku eṉṉum col teṉcollākavumirukkalām. culka (Sulka) eṉṉum vaṭacol maṇamakaṭ koḷḷum paricattaiyē ciṟappāyk kuṟikkum. ulku eṉṉum collēṉum, culka eṉṉum vaṭacolliṉ tiripāṉa cuṅkam eṉṉum collēṉum, tamiḻiṟ paricattaik kuṟippatillai. ulku teṉcollāyiṉ, ulku-culka(va.) cuṅkam eṉṟu tirintiruttal vēṇṭum. teṉ colliṉ tiripāṉa vaṭacolliṉiṉṟu tirinta collum piṟkālat tamiḻil vaḻaṅkum.

ulakat tamiḻk karuttaraṅku mānāṭukaḷ
mutaṟ karuttaraṅku, muṟaippaṭi tamiḻnāṭṭil naṭaipeṟātu, ayalnāṭāṉa malaiyāvil naṭaipeṟṟatu. kaṭcit talaivar palar cuṟṟulāc celvatupōṟ kuṭumpattuṭaṉum aṅkuc ceṉṟaṉar.
iraṇṭām karuttaraṅku ceṉṉaiyil naṭaipeṟṟatu. tamiḻaṉ par palar eṉṉaip paṟṟit tiru. aṇṇātturaiyārkku eḻutiṉatiṉāl, avar tam āṭciyatikārattaip payaṉpaṭutti, tamiḻttāṭkaḷ (Tamil Paper) eṉṉum kaṭaippaṭṭa a-ām kaṭṭuraik kuḻuvil, iṟutiyil eṉ peyaraic cērttuk koṇṭār. eṉakkuriya kuḻu moḻinūl, ataṟkut talaivarā yiruntavar vaṅkap pirāmaṇarāṉa par. caṭṭarci.
tamiḻ valluyiruḷḷa vaḻaṅku moḻiyātalāl, ulakat tamiḻk karuttaraṅku mānāṭu, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷ illāta kālattil avarkaḷ vaḻippaṭṭa oru moḻinūlaṟiñarāṉa tamiḻp pulavar talaimaiyil, tamiḻaruḷḷa nāṭṭil tamiḻilēyē naṭaipeṟal vēṇṭum. akap pakaivarum puṟappakaivarum aṟavē vilakkap paṭal vēṇṭum. kaṭṭuraikaḷellām tamiḻilēyē paṭikkap paṭal vēṇṭum. tamiḻiṟ pēcavō  eḻutavō paṭikkavō āṟṟalillār pārvaiyāḷarāka maṭṭum iruttal vēṇṭum. karuttaraṅku mānāṭṭu maṇṭapattiṟkarukil amaikkappaṭum māperum pantaṟkīḻ, potumakkaḷ amarntu mānāṭṭu nikaḻccikaḷaiyum kaṭṭuraikaḷaiyum urattolip pāṉkaḷ (Loud Speakers) vāyilākak kēṭṭaṟital vēṇṭum. mānāṭu muṭintapiṉ, ataṉ naṭapaṭi(kkai)kaḷ āṅkila aṟikkai vāyilāka ayalnāṭṭārkku aṟivikkappaṭal vēṇṭum. immuṟaiyil em mānāṭum naṭaipeṟavillai.
ceṉṉaik karuttaraṅku mānāṭṭil tamiḻk kaṭṭurai paṭippōrkku vaḻikāṭṭiyāka viṭukkappaṭṭa, ārāyccik kaṭṭuraikaḷukkāṉa viḷakkak kuṟippukaḷ eṉṉum aṟivippil,
yātum ūrē yāvaruṅ kēḷir eṉkiṟa tamiḻ mūturaiyil poruttamāka veḷippaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷa oṉṟē ulakam eṉṉum nam vāḻvu muṟaiyiṉ uyarvu naviṟciyaṟṟa kāṭciyai vaḻaṅkuvatē nammuṭaiya nōkkamāka irukka vēṇṭum.
..................... kaltōṉṟi maṇtōṉṟāk kālattu muṉtōṉṟiya paḻaṅkuṭi eṉpatu pōṉṟa takka ātāramillāta taṟperumai yāṉa kūṟṟukkaḷ, etaiyum  aṟiviyal aṭippaṭaiyil ārāyntu pārkkum nammuṭaiya takutiyilum tiṟamaiyilum ava nampikkai tāṉ ēṟpaṭac ceyyum. eṉṟuḷḷatu iḵtoṉṟē, tirut taṉināyakam ulakat tamiḻp pēravai vaiyāpurikaḷ kūṭṭam eṉpataik kāṭṭap pōtiya cāṉṟām.
ulakat tamiḻp pēravait talaivarāṉa, tamiḻaṟiyāta pērā. pilliyōcā, paṇṭaināḷil camaṟkirutamē intiyap potu moḻiyāyirunta teṉṟār. āriya moḻiyiṉarāṉa tiru. airā vatammakātēvaṉ tamiḻeḻuttaip pirāmiyeḻuttōṭu toṭarpupaṭutti, tamiḻeḻuttum tolkāppiyamum. ki.mu.2-am nūṟṟāṇṭil tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟār. koccait tamiḻ nacciyā rāṉa par.kāmil cuvalepil, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷum cōmacuntara pāratiyārum pāratitācaṉum tamiḻaik keṭuttavar eṉṟu aṟivittār. avar naṟṟiṇai eṉṉum kaṭaikkaḻakac ceyyuṭ paṉuvalaiyum nāluvēli nilam eṉṉum kaṭuṅkoccai nāṭakap pottakattaiyum aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu, tamiḻ tōṉṟiya kālam ki.mu. 1500 eṉṟu kaṇittiruppatum, atu ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakattāl veḷiyiṭappaṭṭiruppatum, iṉṟait tamiḻar māperum pētaiyar kūṭṭam eṉpataiyē kāṭṭum.
pāricu mānakaril naṭanta 3-ām ulakat tamiḻk karuttaraṅku mānāṭum, cuṟṟulāk kūṭṭamākavē naṭantatu. atiṟ kalantu koṇṭa kaṭcit talaivaraiyellām takuti vāyntavarē yeṉṟu, poṟuppaṟṟataṉamāy eḻutiṉār oru poṟuppu vāynta tamiḻp pērāciriyar. cintu veḷi nākarikam tiraviṭaratu eṉpatu par.kāmil cuvelapilāl maṟukkappaṭṭu, ataṉ tīrppu tiru. airāvatam makātēvaṉiṭam oppaṭaikkappaṭṭatu. ulakat tamiḻp pēravaiyiṉ nōkkam ārāycciyēyaṉṟi, tamiḻai vaḷarppatō parappuvatō aṉṟeṉṟu tiruttaṉināyakattāl veḷippaṭaiyākac collappaṭṭatu.
paṭṭam peṟuvatum pataviyuyarttuvatum perum poruḷīṭṭu vatumē vaṇikap pulavar kuṟikkōḷ.

vaṇikap pulavar cilar, paṇṭārakarp paṭṭam peṟṟa aḷavil, tammai aṉaittu maṟintavareṉṟum ellām vallunareṉṟum iṟappa matittu, palkalaik kaḻakat tuṇaik kaṇkāṇakaraiyum amaiccaraiyum ēmāṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉar. tāṉ aṟiyātāṉ eṉpatai aṟiyātāṉ muṭṭāḷ, avaṉai viṭṭoḻi. par. kāmil cuvelapil, tam Lectures on Historical Grammer on Tamil eṉṉum āṅkilac cuvaṭiyil, koccait tamiḻaiyum ulaka vaḻakkut tamiḻoṭu cērkkum muppērāciriyarai vāṉaḷāvap pukaḻntu, “the three most eminent Tamil scholars of our time” eṉṟu (pak. 12) kuṟittirukkiṉṟār. ammūvaruḷ, oruvar moḻi nūlaiyē aṟiyātavar; oruvar mēlaiyar moḻinūlkaḷaip paṭittu maṭṭum iruppavar; oruvar camaṟkiruta aṭippaṭaiyil tamiḻāyntavar. innūṟṟāṇṭil mupperuntamiḻp pulavar eṉṟu collat takkār, maṟaimalaiyaṭikaḷum cāminātaiyarum kā. cuppiramaṇiyap piḷḷaiyumē.
camaṟkirutap pērāciriyarāṉa par. parōvum par.emeṉōvum ciṟanta aṟiñarēṉum, tamiḻaic ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻakat tamiḻakara mutali koṇṭum, tiraviṭa moḻikaḷai avaṟṟiluḷḷa akara mutalikaḷ koṇṭum, paṭittavarēyaṉṟi, tiraviṭamoḻi kaṭkut tāyāṉa tamiḻai āḻntu āyntavarallar. ataṉāl, āriyamoḻi tirāviṭattiṉiṉṟu 500 coṟkaḷ pōṟ kaṭaṉ koṇṭuḷḷatē yaṉṟi, āriya moḻiyamaippiṟkuḷ tirāviṭam pukavillai yeṉṟu, muṭipu koṇṭu viṭṭaṉar. ettuṇaip pēraṟiñarāyiṉum, aṭippaṭai tavaṟāyiṉ muṭipum tavaṟākavē yirukkum. mēlai moḻinūlār ellārum, tamiḻar naṇṇilak kaṭaṟkarai nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟu vantēṟikaḷ eṉṉum tavaṟāṉa koḷkaiyiṉarē.
oru nāṭṭu ilakkiyattai ayalār kaṟṟu atil tērcci peṟalām. āyiṉ, ataṉ moḻiyai, atait tāymoḻiyākak koṇṭavarōṭu piḷḷaip paruvattiṉiṉṟu neruṅkip paḻakiṉāloḻiya, orē talaimuṟaiyil avar pōl muṟṟak kaṟka muṭiyātu.
ōyvu peṟṟa camaṟkirutap pērāciriyāṉa parōvum (T.Burrow) emeṉōvum (M.B. Emeneau) tokuttuḷḷa tiraviṭa akara mutali, tiraviṭac coṟ piṟappiyal akara mutali (A Dravidian Etymological Dictionary) eṉṟu peyar peṟṟiruppiṉum, uṇmaiyil ōr oppiyal akara mutaliyē (A Comparative Lexicon).
kālṭuvelār karuttiṟ kiṇaṅkat tamiḻai āḻntu kaṟṟut tiraviṭamoḻikaḷaik koṭivaḻi muṟaippaṭi (Geneological method) ārāyātu, iṟṟai vaṇṇaṉai moḻinūl (Descriptive Linguistics) pōl oppiyal muṟaippaṭi (Comparative method) maṭṭum nōkki, tamiḻiluḷḷa tavaṟāṉa ceṉṉaip palkalaik kaḻaka akara mutaliyaiyum, tamiḻait tāymoḻiyākap payaṉ paṭuttik koṇṭē atai iyalpāka veṟukkum pirāmaṇak kulattaic cērnta (P.S.) cuppiramaṇiya cāttiriyār eḻutiya tolkāppiya eḻuttatikāra collatikārak kuṟippuraikaḷaiyum, aḷavai nūlkaḷallāta piṟavaṟṟaiyum, tamiḻaṟiyātavar tokutta tiraviṭa moḻi yakara mutalikaḷaiyumē, aṭippaṭaiyākak koṇṭu tokuttataṉāl, avar akaramutali piṉvarumāṟu pala piḻaipāṭukaḷ malintuḷḷatu.
(1)	tamiḻ kumarināṭṭil tōṉṟiya teṉṉum aṭippaṭai yuṇmaiyai aṟiyāmai.
(2)	camaṟkirutam muntiyateṉṟum ataṉāl tamiḻ vaḷam paṭuttap paṭṭateṉṟum oru muṟkōḷ (prejudice) uṭaimai.
(3)	aṅkaṇam (anganam) eṉpatai ankanam eṉṟum, añcu (anju) eṉpatai ancu eṉṟum, āṇṭi (andi) eṉpatai anti eṉṟum, antaṇaṉ (andanam) eṉpatai antanan eṉṟum, ampu (ambu) eṉpatai ampu eṉṟum, tavaṟāka  āṅkilattil eḻuttup peyarttuḷḷamai.
(4)	pala teṉ coṟkaḷai āriyamāka allatu camaṟkiruta mākak kāṭṭiyuḷḷamai.

(8)	āyirak kaṇakkāṉa teṉ coṟkaḷ viṭupaṭṭup pōṉa ceṉṉai akara mutaliyiluḷḷa tamiḻc coṟkaḷum, ellām kāṭṭappaṭāmai.

(10)	vaṭa intiyac coṟkaḷaiyellām camaṟkiruta meṉṟu koṇṭuḷḷamai.
ivaṟṟāl, iṟṟai mēlait tamiḻaṟiñaruḷ talaiciṟanta pērāciriyaṉmārum paṇṭārakaṉmārum tamiḻatikārikaḷallar eṉṟum, avarkaḷ kalantu koṇṭataṉāl ulakat tamiḻk karuttaraṅku mānāṭukaḷ ciṟappuṟṟuviṭā eṉṟum, teṟṟeṉat terintu koḷka.
kālṭuvelār tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ oppiyal ilakkaṇa 3-ām patippil, muṉṉuraiyiluḷḷa tiraviṭa moḻikaḷiṉ mutupaḻañ cāṉṟukaḷ eṉṉum pakutiyil, ‘Komaria Akron’ eṉṉum talaippiṉ kīḻuḷḷa aṭikkuṟippil (pak.96).
“Compare Cymri (Wales). “It is stated that the original home of the Cwmry, Cumri or Cymry, was in the Southern Hindustan, the Southern extremity of which, Cape Comorin, takes its name from the same root.” - From a Historical Souvenir issued on the occasion of the meeting of the British Medical Association at Swansea, 1903-Editors.” 
eṉṟu kaṉam vayaṟṟu aiyarum (Rev. J.L. Wyatt, M.A.) (T.) irāmakiruṭṭiṇa piḷḷaiyum (B.A.) kuṟittiruttalaik kūrntu nōkkuka.
palkuḻuvum pāḻceyyu muṭpakaiyum vēntalaikkum kolkuṟumpum tamiḻnāṭṭil mikkiruppatāl, avaṟṟai yellām nīkki, evarukkum añcātu naṭunilaiyil niṉṟu muṉṉōr muṟaiyil tamiḻai vaḷarttaṟku, 1969ām āṇṭu ulakat tamiḻk kaḻakam tōṟṟuvikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
ulakam
ulakam-lōka (loka)- vē.
ul-ulam = uruṭci, tiraṭci, uruṇṭa kal.
ulamvā-ulamā. ulamarutal = cuḻalutal, uḻalutal.
ulakkai = uruṇṭu nīṇṭa peruntaṭi.
ulaṇṭu = uruṇṭu nīṇṭa puḻu.
ulam-ulavu. ulavutal = cuṟṟutal, tirital.
ulā = cuṟṟi varutal, aracaṉ valamāka nakaraic cuṟṟi varutal,
ataip pāṭiya paṉuval.
ulāvutal = cuṟṟi varutal. ulāttutal = cuṟṟi varutal.
ulāñcutal = talai cuṟṟutal.
ulam-ulavu-ulaku. o.nō : puṟam-puṟavu-puṟaku.
ulaku=uruṇṭaiyāṉatu allatu cuṟṟi varuvatu.
o.nō : koḷ-kōḷ. koṭṭutal = cuṟṟi varutal.
ulaku-ulakam.  (va.va.)
mūvulakam, īrēḻulakam, ulakamellām eṉṉum vaḻakkukkaḷ, ulakamam eṉṉum colliṉ potumaiyaik kuṟikkum. katiravaṉ, tiṅkaḷ eṉṉum iru cuṭariṉ uruṇṭa vaṭivum cūḻvarukait tōṟṟamum ulakaṅkaḷ potuvāy uruṇṭaiyā ṉavai eṉṟum peruveḷiyil vaṭṭamākac cuṟṟi varukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟum, iru karuttukkaḷait tōṟṟuvittaṉa.
vaṭamoḻiyil lōka eṉṉuñ coṟkup pārkkappaṭṭa iṭam eṉṟē poruḷ kūṟuvar. aṅṅaṉamē poruṭkāraṇamuṅ kāṭṭuvar. lōk = pār. E. look.
itaṉāl, ulakattaik kuṟikkum lōka eṉṉum vaṭacol teṉcolliṉ tiripeṉpatu teḷḷat teḷivām.
ulakam eṉṉum colvaḻakku toṉṟu toṭṭatu.
kāla mulakam   (tol. 541).  (va.va:96)
ulaku : ul-ulā = cuṟṟivarutal. ul-ula-ulavu-ulāvu. ulāvutal = vaḷaital, vaṭṭamātal, cuṟṟutal, tirital, ulavu-ulaku = uruṇṭaiyāyiruppatu. cuṟṟivaruvatu.
ulaku-ulakam-lōka (va.) vaṭamoḻiyil lōka eṉṉum coṟkuk kūṟappaṭum poruṭ karaṇiyam pārkkappaṭuvatu eṉpatē. atu look eṉṉum āṅkilac colloṭu toṭar puṭaiyatu. atu vēṟu; ulakattaik kuṟikkum teṉ colliṉ tiripu vēṟu.
kālam ulakam uyirē yuṭampē (tol. 541) eṉṟu āciyar eṭuttōtutalāl ivaiyellān teṉcollēyeṉṟu nacciṉārkkiṉiyaruṅ kūṟutal kāṇka.  (ti.ma : 738)
ulakam 
ulakam eṉṟatu (ulakat tōṭoṭṭa oḻukal) varaiyaṟaip paṭṭa iṭavākupeyar.
aṟanūlkaḷil ellā oḻukka muṟaikaḷum collappaṭāmai yālum kālantōṟum paḻaiyaṉa kaḻitalum putiyaṉa pukutalum nikaḻtalāṉum oḻukka vakaiyil uyarn tōraip piṉpaṟṟutal iṉṟiyamaiyātatu āyiṟṟu. kalviyāl aṟivum, aṟivāl oḻukkamum payaṉātaliṉ uyarntārōṭu porunta oḻukutal vēṇṭum. oḻukutal piṉpaṟṟi oḻukutal.  (ti.mi. 110.)
ulaka mutaṉmoḻik koḷkai
1. māntaṉ tōṉṟiyatu kumarināṭāyirukkalām eṉpatu
itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷum kāraṇaṅkaḷum :
(1)	kumari nāṭṭiṉ paḻaimai.
(2)	hekkēl kiḷēṟṟar mutaliyōr ilemuriyā māntaṉ tōṉṟiya iṭamākak kūṟiyiruttal.
(3)	kumarināṭṭu moḻiyiṉ toṉmaiyum muṉmaiyum.
(4)	teṉṉāṭṭup peruṅkaṭalkōṭ katai ulaka muḻutum vaḻaṅkal.
(5)	makkaḷ kiḻakkiliruntu vantār eṉṟu yūta carittiraṅ kūṟal1.
(6)	toṉmarapiṉarāṉa māntar perumpālum teṉñālattiliruttal.
(7)	kumarināṭirunta iṭam ñālattiṉ naṭumaiyamā yiruttal.
(8)	kuḷiriṉum vemmaiyē makkaṭkēṟṟal.
(9)	teṉñālattiṉ vaḷamai.
ulakattiṟ kiṭaikkum poṉṉum, vayiramum, perumpālum teṉṉāppirikkā, teṉṉintiyā, teṉkaṇṭam (Australia) ākiya iṭaṅkaḷilēyē eṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
(10)	mutal māntaṉ vāḻakkūṭiya kaṉimarakkā (ētēṉ) teṉ ñālak kuṟiñci nāṭukaḷilēyē kāṇak kūṭiyatā yiruttal.
kuṟippu : kiṟittuva viṭaiyūḻiyar kāṉāṉ nāṭṭai ñālattiṉ maiyam eṉṟu kūṟuvatu tavaṟākum. annāṭu naḷḷikai (Equator)¡F vaṭakkē 30ām 40ām pākaikaṭ kiṭaiyiluḷḷatu. kumarināṭṭiṭamō naḷḷikaiyiṉ mēlēyē uḷḷatu. mēlum, paṇṭai ñālattil teṉpākattilēyē nilam mikkirunta teṉṟum, kāṉāṉ nāṭṭu nilam nīrkkīḻ iruntateṉṟum aṟiya vēṇṭum.
ātiyākamattil māntaṉ paṭaippai yūta carittirattōṭu iṇaittuk kūṟiyirukkiṟatu.
yūtar mutalmakkaḷ vakuppārallar eṉpataṟkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ:
(i)	paḻaiya ēṟpāṭṭil 4000 āṇṭuc caritaiyē kūṟap paṭṭuḷḷamai.
itupōtu 6000 āṇṭukaṭku mēṟpaṭṭa maraṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa vākac collappaṭuvatu. tamiḻmoḻi tōṉṟiya kālam ev vakaiyilum ki.mu. 5000 āṇṭukaṭkup piṟpaṭātu.
(ii)	kāyīṉ taṉṉaip palar kalleṟivār eṉṟu kūṟiyiruttal.
(iii)	makkaḷ kiḻakkiliruntu vantāreṉṟu yūta carittiraṅ kūṟal.
(iv)	yūtar tēva puttiraraik kaṇṭāreṉal.
tēva puttirareṉṟu ulakil collattakkavar veḷḷaiyarāṉa āriyar. āriyarait tēvareṉṟu collattakkavar teṉ ṉāṭṭiliruntu ceṉṟa karupparāyiruntiruttal vēṇṭum.
(v)	ulaka muḻutum orē moḻi vaḻaṅkiṟṟeṉṟu ātiyāka mattiṟ kūṟiyiruttalum, epirēya moḻi ulaka mutaṉ moḻiyātaṟ kēṟkāmaiyum.
(vi)	epirēya moḻiyil pala tamiḻccoṟkaḷ citaintu kiṭattal.
kā : āp (appaṉ), ām (ammai), nūṉ (mīṉ), vāv (vaḷaivu), mēm (mēkam = nīr), pē (vāy).  mēkam eṉpatu mēlēyuḷḷa nīr eṉṟu poruḷpaṭum tamiḻccollē. mē (mēl)+kam(nīr) = mēkam.
ātām (māntaṉ) eṉṉum mutal epirēyappeyar ātōm (civappu) eṉpatiṉ tirivākac collappaṭukiṟatu. ātōm eṉpatu arattam (civappu) eṉṉum tamiḻccolliṉ tiripā yirukkalām.
(vii)	ātiyākamattiṟ paṭaippaippaṟṟik kūṟumiṭattē, vāram eṉṉum eḻunāḷaḷavaik kūṟiyiruttal.
pakalum iravum cērnta nāḷ eṉṉum aḷavu mutalilirun tuḷḷatu. āṉāl, eḻukōḷkaḷaik kaṇṭupiṭitta piṉ, avaṟ ṟiṉ peyarāl uṇṭāṉa vāram eṉṉum aḷavu piṟkālattatu.
mōcē ulaka carittira maṟintavarallar. avar akkālattu makka ḷiṉ aṟivunilaikkēṟṟapaṭi, paḻaimai muṟaiyiṟ paṭaippaip paṟṟik kūṟiṉār. iyēcuperumāṉ tāmē tiruvāy malarn taruḷiṉatē, kiṟittuvar aiyamiṉṟik koḷḷattakkatu. kaṭavuḷ niṉaittavaḷavil ellāvaṟṟaiyum paṭaippavar. ataṟku vāyccollum ēḻunāḷum vēṇṭiyatillai.
2. tamiḻ ulaka mutaṟperumoḻiyā yirukkalāmeṉpatu
itaṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷum kāraṇaṅkaḷum:
(1)	tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ paḻaimai.
(2)	tamiḻiṉ paḻaimai.
(3)	tamiḻiṉ eḷiya volikaḷ.
(4)	tamiḻil iṭu kuṟiccollum cuṭṭacaiyum (Definite Article) illāmai.
(5)	tamiḻil oṭṭuccoṟkaḷ cilavāyiruttal.
(6)	ammai appaṉ eṉṉum tamiḻ muṟaippeyarkaḷ pala vaṭivil ulakamoḻikaḷ palavaṟṟil vaḻaṅkal.
(7)	tamiḻccoṟkaḷ cilavō palavō ulaka moḻikaḷ ellāvaṟṟilu miruttal.
(8)	mummoḻik kulaṅkaḷiṉ ciṟappiyalpum oruciṟitu tamiḻiṟ kāṇappaṭal.
vaṭamoḻikkuriya nīṭṭaṟpuṇarcci (tīrkkacanti) tamiḻiluḷḷamai.
cittiyak kulattiṟkuc ciṟanta uyiropput tiripu (Harmonious Sequence of Vowels) potiyil (potu+il), ciṟiyilai (ciṟu+ilai) mutaliya toṭarmoḻikaḷil uḷatu.
cīṉattil orē col iṭavēṟṟumaiyāl vevvēṟu col vakaiyākum. tamiḻilum iḵtuṇṭu.
kā : poṉ (aḻakitu) - peyarccol. (atu) poṉ viṉaic col. poṉ (vaḷaiyal) - peyareccam. poṉ (viḷainta kaḷattūr) - viṉaiyeccam.
piṟamoḻikaḷil uḷḷa orumai irumai paṉmai eṉṉum eṇpākupāṭṭiṟku, avaṉ avar avarkaḷ, allatu oru cila pala eṉṉum vaḻakkukaḷ mūlamāyirukkalām.
(9)	tamiḻil olikkuṟippāyuṅ kuṟippoliyāyu muḷḷavai piṟamoḻikaḷil collāy vaḻaṅkal.
fh : juju - tear, jfjf - jà (t.), ucu - raḷā, caḷappu - saliva, cekkak kekka - L.cachinno, V.E.cachinnation.
(10)	ayaṉmoḻic coṟkaḷ palavaṟṟiṟku vēr tamiḻi liruttal.
mukkula moḻikaḷilumuḷḷa cuṭṭu viṉāc coṟkaḷiṉ aṭikaḷ tamiḻiliruppatu mukkiyamākak kavaṉikkattakkatu.
(11)	eḻukiḻamaip peyarkaḷum paṉṉīrōraip peyar kaḷum, āriya moḻikaḷil tamiḻccoṟkaḷiṉ moḻi peyarppāyiruttal.
paṇṭaittamiḻar vāṉūl vallōrāyiruntamai. avar nāḷiṉ (nāṇmīṉiṉ) peyarāl oru kiḻamaiyaḷavaiyum, eḻukōḷkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷāl oru vāra aḷavaiyum, matiyiṉ peyarāl oru mātavaḷavaiyum, cūriyaṉ peyarāl ōr āṇṭaḷavaiyum kuṟittiruttalē. avaratu vāṉūlaṟiviṟkuc ciṟanta cāṉṟām. ivvaḷavukaḷ mutaṉmutaṟ kumarināṭṭilēyē tōṉṟiyavai.
āṅkilattil uḷḷa vāra nāṭ peyarkaḷil, Sunday, Monday, Tuesday, Saturday eṉṉum nāṉkum tamiḻppeyarkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppā yirukkiṉṟaṉa.
Tues eṉpatu zeus eṉpatiṉ maṟuvaṭivamākak kūṟappaṭukiṟatu. ivai muṟaiyē cevvāy cēyōṉ eṉṉum peyarkaḷaic collālum poruḷālum ottirukkiṉṟaṉa. zues kirakkap perunteyvam. kirēkkar tamiḻnāṭṭil mikappaḻaṅ kālatti lēyē kuṭiyiruntaṉar. avarai yavaṉar eṉṟu tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟum. yavaṉam eṉpatu kirēkka nāṭṭiṉ  peyarkaḷuḷ oṉṟu. pāṇṭiyaṉukkum kirēkka maṉṉarukkum kayalmīṉ ciṉṉamā yiruntatum. tamiḻarukkum kirēkkarukkum pala paḻakkavaḻakkaṅkaḷ potuvāyiruntatum iṅkuk kavaṉikkat takkaṉa.
ilattīṉil vaḻaṅkiya paṉṉīr oraippeyarkaḷum tamiḻp peyarkaḷiṉ moḻipeyarppākavē yirukkiṉṟaṉa.
I. Aries (ram), ii. Taurus (bull), iii. Gemini (twins). iv. Cancer (crab), v. Leo. (lion), vi. Virgo (virgin), vii. Libra (balance), viii. Scorpio (scorpion), ix. Sagittarius (archer or bow), x. Capricorn (the goat - horned = shark), xi. Aquarius (water-bearer = pitcher) xii. Pisces (fish).
(12)	tamiḻil mutal vēṟṟumaik kurupiṉmai.
(13)	tamiḻ vēṟṟumai yurupukaḷellām piṉṉoṭṭuc coṟkaḷāyiruttal.
(14)	coṟṟoṭaril tamiḻccoṉmuṟai iyalpāyiruttal.
paṉṉiraṇṭu eṉṉum coṉmuṟai pattōṭu iraṇṭu cērntatu eṉpataik kuṟippatākum. vaṭamoḻiyil tuvātacam eṉṉum muṟai iyaṟkaikku māṟāṉatākum.
(15)	tamiḻ ulaka mutaṉmoḻi āyvukku niṟṟal.
tamiḻoṭu piṟa moḻikaḷ ovvāmaikkuk kāraṇaṅkaḷ
(1)	tamiḻiṉ palvēṟu nilaikaḷil makkaḷ pirintu pōṉamai.
tamiḻ kuṟippoli nilaiyi liruntapōtum, acai nilaiyi liruntapōtum, puṇarnilaiyiliruntapōtum, pakucoṉṉilaiyi liruntapōtum, tokunilaiyilirunta pōtumākap paṟpala camayaṅkaḷil, kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟum makkaḷ kiḻakkum mēṟkum vaṭakkumākap pirintupōyirukkiṉṟaṉar.
(2)	kuṟippoli nilaiyilum acainilaiyilum pirinta māntar avaṟṟai aṭipṭaiyākak koṇṭu, pal vēṟu coṟkaḷai amaittukkoṇṭamai.
(3)	pirintupōṉa makkaḷ mūlaccoṟkaḷai maṟantuviṭṭup putuccoṟkaḷai ākkikkoṇṭamai.
(4)	taṭpaveppanilai uṇavu mutaliyavaṟṟāl uṟupput tirintu, ataṉāl uccaripput tirintamai.
kā : tōkai - Pers. tawus. Gk. taos, L.pavo, A.S. pawe, E. pea-peacock. nr-nrî-(nr¡F) - A.S. coc, E.cock. o.nō : nā-nāvu-nākku.
(5)	perumpāṟ coṟkaḷ pōli marūu citaivu mutaliya muṟaikaḷil tirintamai.
(6)	mūlamoḻiyi lillāta volikaḷ tōṉṟiṉamai.
(7)	mūlamoḻi yilakkaṇattiṉiṉṟum vēṟupaṭṭa ilak kaṇam eḻutappeṟṟamai.
(8)	periyōrai matittal kāraṇamākac coṟkaḷai māṟṟal.
palanīciyat (Polynesian) tīvukaḷil tepi (Tepi) eṉṟoru vaḻakkamuḷḷatu. ataṉpaṭi, aracaṉ peyarāvatu ataṉ pāka māvatu varukiṉṟa coṟkaḷaiyellām māṟṟi viṭukiṉṟaṉar. ivvaḻakkam teṉṉāppirikkāvilum uḷḷatām.
vaṭamoḻi ulaka mutaṉmoḻiyāka muṭiyāmai
moḻikaḷiṉ iyalpaiyaṟiyāta palar, vaṭamoḻi ulaka mutaṉmoḻiyāyirukkalāmeṉṟu karutukiṉṟaṉar.
ulakil mutalāvatu tōṉṟiya tiruntiya moḻi tamiḻē. kumarināṭṭil tamiḻ kuṟippoli nilaiyiliruntapōtu pirinta makkaḷ, āppirikkā, teṉkaṇṭam, amerikkā mutaliya iṭaṅkaṭkum, acainilaiyiṟ pirinta makkaḷ kaṭāram cīṉam vaṭa āciyā airōppā  mutaliya iṭaṅkaṭkum ceṉṟatākat teri kiṉṟatu. acainilaiyiṟ pirinta makkaḷiṉ moḻikaḷē, turēṉi yam allatu cittiyam eṉṟu kūṟappaṭum kuṭumpattavai.
pālṭik kaṭaṟpāṅkaril, turēṉiyattiṉ tiripākavē āriyam tōṉṟiyirukkiṉṟatu. pālṭikkiṉ vaṭapākaṅkaḷil piṉṉiyam (Finnish) eṉṉum turēṉiya moḻi vaḻaṅkuvatum, cuvīṭiyam (Swedish) ṭēṉiyam (Danish) mutaliya moḻikaḷil tamiḻc coṟkaḷ iruppatum, jermāṉiyattiluḷḷa cila coṟkaḷ āriya ilakkiya moḻiccoṟkaḷ cilavaṟṟiṟku mūlamāyum tamiḻukku nerukkamāyumiruppatum, āṭṭō cirētar kūṟiyirup patum ik koḷkaikkuc cāṉṟukaḷām.
āriyañceṉṟa tamiḻccoṟkaḷ piṉvarumāṟu mūvakaiya:
(1)	āriyariṉ muṉṉōr kumarināṭṭiṉiṉṟu pirintu pōṉapōtē uṭaṉceṉṟavai.
